### [102] [Quantum typicality survives non-Abelian gauge constraints: exact analytical prediction confirmed in $SU(2)$ lattice gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27402)
*Zhi-Wei Wang,Samuel L. Braunstein*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在SU(2)格点规范理论中，量子典型性（子系统间关联的普遍缺失）在物理希尔伯特空间仍然成立，非阿贝尔约束并未注入支持几何的纠缠；Kogut-Susskind哈密顿量从确定几何态产生关联，而通用态仅呈现均值回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证涌现时空理论的核心前提——量子典型性是否在非阿贝尔规范理论的物理希尔伯特空间中仍然成立，因为非阿贝尔约束理论上可能注入支持几何结构的纠缠。

Method: 在二维环面上进行SU(2)格点规范理论计算（物理维度最高达4,193），分析严格不相交链之间的典型互信息，并将其与微正则基线和Haar随机涨落结合的无参数解析预测进行对比。

Result: 典型互信息与解析预测完全匹配，表明量子典型性在规范约束下仍然保持；Kogut-Susskind哈密顿量仅在确定几何态（如电真空）中产生关联，而通用态仅表现出向均值的回归。

Conclusion: 关联增长的箭头需要非通用的初始条件，规范理论的物理希尔伯特空间并不天然具备支持涌现几何的纠缠结构。

Abstract: Arguments for emergent spacetime require that quantum typicality, the generic absence of inter-subsystem correlations, persists on the physical Hilbert space of a gauge theory, where non-Abelian constraints could in principle inject geometry-supporting entanglement. Using $SU(2)$ lattice gauge theory on two-dimensional tori ($d_{\mathrm{phys}}$ up to $4{,}193$), we show that it does: the typical mutual information between strictly disjoint links matches an exact parameter-free analytical prediction combining a microcanonical baseline with Haar-random fluctuations. The Kogut-Susskind Hamiltonian generates correlations from states of definite geometry (such as the electric vacuum), while generic states show only regression to the mean, establishing that the arrow of correlation growth requires a non-generic initial condition.

</details>


### [103] [Reservoir-independent lossless charging and protected storage of an open quantum battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27403)
*Asad Ali,H. Kuniyil,M. I Hussain,M. T Rahim,Saif Al-Kuwari,James Q. Quach*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过暗态结构实现无损耗量子电池充电的方案：在三能级系统中，利用反绝热驱动场精确抵消损耗态的残余激发源，使辐射衰减态始终保持空置，从而实现与耗散无关的无损充电，充电功率仅受量子速度极限限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子电池通过损耗中间态充电时面临充电速度与耗散之间的结构性权衡，本文旨在克服这一权衡，实现无损耗的快速充电。

Method: 采用三能级驱动系统，通过代数抵消机制：辐射衰减态由单一亮态振幅驱动，反绝热场消除唯一的残余源，使损耗态保持空置。利用暗态结构实现系统-浴耦合的完全解耦，并用阻尼赝模处理验证任意谱密度下的精确性。

Result: 实现了无损充电——不发射任何光子；充电功率由驱动振幅而非耗散决定；无损性对任意谱密度（马尔可夫或非马尔可夫）均成立；将快速辐射自放电转化为慢速亚稳态寿命；残差仅与控制误差的二次方相关。

Conclusion: 该方案反转了耗散工程充电的范式——不利用奇异点或存储记忆作为资源，而是通过暗态结构使损耗态完全不占据，从根本上规避耗散。提供了在碱土金属原子、囚禁离子、transmon和缺陷中心等平台上的实验参数。

Abstract: A quantum battery charged through a lossy intermediate state faces a structural trade-off between charging speed and dissipation. We show that an exact algebraic cancellation removes it in a driven three-level cell: the radiatively decaying state is fed by a single bright amplitude, and a counterdiabatic field annuls the lone residual source that drives it, holding the lossy state identically empty. Charging is then lossless -- not one photon is emitted through the bridge -- at any one-photon detuning, coupling, linewidth, and speed down to the rotating-wave limit, with no adiabatic elimination, so the charging power is bounded by the drive amplitude (a quantum speed limit) rather than by dissipation. Crucially, this losslessness is independent of the reservoir: because the dark sector never engages the system-bath coupling, the emission vanishes exactly for an arbitrary spectral density, Markovian or not, as an exact damped-pseudomode treatment confirms to machine precision across all memory times. The entire non-Hermitian structure -- a Markovian second-order exceptional point that reservoir memory promotes to a third-order one, and the attendant dissipation phase diagram -- lives in the bright sector, from which the protocol is by construction exempt. This inverts dissipation-engineered charging, where an exceptional point or reservoir memory is a resource; here the lossy sector is never populated at all. The same dark-state structure protects the stored charge, converting fast radiative self-discharge into the slow metastable lifetime, with residuals quadratic in the control error. We detail experimental requirements and representative parameters for neutral alkaline-earth atoms, trapped ions, transmons, and defect centers.

</details>


### [104] [Compression-Driven Anomaly Detection in Brain MRI Using an Interpretable Quantum Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27411)
*Santanu Ganguly,Xing Liang,Dimitrios Makris*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于量子自动编码器（QAE）的压缩驱动异常检测方法，用于脑部MRI数据。通过角度编码将图像块映射到量子态，利用变分编码器-解码器架构和辅助垃圾量子位丢弃信息。在公开脑部MRI数据集上，切片级ROC-AUC约0.95，块级约0.813，优于经典自编码器和PCA基线。分析发现编码器-解码器存在明显不对称性，异常检测主要源于编码器的结构化信息压缩而非参数幅度或解码器表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子自动编码器在医学影像异常检测中的应用潜力，利用量子压缩特性实现基于不可压缩性的异常检测机制，提供可解释且可控的决策支持工具。

Method: 采用角度编码将图像块映射为量子态，构建变分量子编码器-解码器架构，通过辅助垃圾量子位实现信息丢弃；以压缩程度作为异常评分依据，正常数据易压缩而异常数据抵抗压缩。

Result: 切片级ROC-AUC约0.95，块级ROC-AUC约0.813，优于经典自编码器和PCA基线；量子自动编码器生成的空间定位异常热图与肿瘤区域对齐；编码器-解码器存在显著不对称性。

Conclusion: 量子自动编码器提供了一种基于不可压缩性的可解释、可控异常检测机制，具备清晰的压缩-重建权衡操作区间，支持原则性阈值选择，展现了在量子机器学习中研究压缩动力学的潜力。

Abstract: We study a quantum autoencoder (QAE) for compression-driven anomaly detection in brain MRI data. The approach leverages angle encoding to map image patches into quantum states, followed by a variational encoder-decoder architecture trained to discard information via auxiliary trash qubits. Anomaly scores reflect the degree to which inputs resist compression relative to normal data, with higher scores corresponding to deviations from the learned normal manifold. Evaluated on publicly available brain MRI DICOM datasets, the method achieves a slice-level ROC-AUC of approximately 0.95 and a patch-level ROC-AUC of approximately 0.813, outperforming classical autoencoder and PCA baselines. Analysis of the learned parameters reveals a pronounced encoder-decoder asymmetry, where effective anomaly detection arises from structured information compression within the encoder rather than increased parameter magnitude or decoder expressivity. This results in a controlled compression-reconstruction trade-off with a clear operating regime that supports principled threshold selection. Qualitative evaluation further shows that the QAE produces spatially localized anomaly heatmaps aligned with tumorous regions. The results, supported by promising baseline performances, demonstrate that quantum autoencoders provide an interpretable and controllable mechanism for anomaly detection based on incompressibility with respect to a learned latent representation. This work highlights the potential of quantum autoencoders as a principled tool for studying compression dynamics in quantum machine learning, with promising implications for decision support in medical imaging workflows.

</details>


### [105] [Engineering of non-Hermitian interactions in digital qudit quantum simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27424)
*Matteo M. Wauters,Paolo Boschetto,Edoardo Ballini,Alberto Biella,Philipp Hauke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用qudit量子模拟器中的混合幺正-投影演化，通过精心设计的测量将动力学限制在Zeno子空间，从而在赝自旋1/2系综中实现具有k体非厄米相互作用的有效非厄米哈密顿量，并以qutrit链为例进行了理论验证和数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米哈密顿量的受控工程在量子模拟中仍然具有挑战性，尤其是当非厄米部分描述k体相互作用时。现有的理论框架虽然完善，但缺乏有效的实验实现路径。

Method: 采用qudit量子模拟器中的混合幺正-投影演化方案，通过设计合适的测量将动力学限制在Zeno子空间，从而在赝自旋1/2系综中实现目标非厄米哈密顿量。以qutrit链为例，推导了监测qutrit演化与目标非厄米哈密顿量之间的解析关系，并通过数值模拟验证有效描述。

Result: 在Zeno子空间内，动力学由赝自旋1/2系综的有效非厄米哈密顿量主导，该哈密顿量可继承与完整qutrit链相同连通性的非厄米两体相互作用。通过数值模拟验证了代表性模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方案为在实验相关的多能级量子平台（包括俘获离子和超导电路）中实现一大类相互作用的非厄米多体哈密顿量提供了建设性路径。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian Hamiltonians are a fascinating class of many-body models that describe the effective dynamics of quantum systems interacting with the environment through particle, energy, or information exchange. Although their theoretical framework is well established, the controlled engineering of such Hamiltonians in the context of quantum simulations remains challenging, even more so when the non-Hermitian part describes a $k$-body interaction. Qudit quantum simulators offer a compelling framework to implement such models. We theoretically investigate the dynamics of a one-dimensional chain of qudits undergoing hybrid unitary-projective evolution, where suitably designed measurements constrain the dynamics to a Zeno subspace. As we illustrate for the case of qutrits, within the Zeno subspace the dynamics is governed by an effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian for an ensemble of pseudo-spins $1/2$, which can inherit non-Hermitian two-body interactions with the same connectivity as the full qutrit chain. We derive an analytical relation linking the monitored qutrits' evolution to a desired target non-Hermitian Hamiltonian and validate the effective description through numerical simulations of a representative model. Our scheme provides a constructive route for the realization of a large class of interacting non-Hermitian many-body Hamiltonians in experimentally relevant multilevel quantum platforms, including trapped ions and superconducting circuits.

</details>


### [106] [Multi-parameter two-photon polarimetry at the quantum limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27428)
*Joseph Niblo,Luca Maggio,Russell M. J. Brooks,Joseph Ho,Vincenzo Tamma,Alessandro Fedrizzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实验协议，使用约200个光子对，在极化参数空间中同时逼近量子Cramér-Rao精度界，解决了量子测量在多参数同时估计中的不兼容性问题，对暗源极化传感（如X射线天文学或光敏样品）具有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量在同时估计多个参数时存在固有的不兼容性，这阻碍了光子量子计量学在多参数同时估计中的优势扩展。

Method: 设计了一种实验协议，使用约200个光子对，在极化参数空间中同时逼近量子Cramér-Rao精度界。

Result: 在广泛的参数空间内，同时逼近了两个极化参数的QCRB，仅需约200个光子对。

Conclusion: 该协议对暗源极化传感（如X射线天文学或光敏样品）具有显著优势，展示了光子量子计量学在多参数同时估计中的潜力。

Abstract: Photonic quantum metrology has demonstrated advantages in precision and resource efficiency for a wide range of applications, with several schemes approaching the fundamental quantum Cramér-Rao precision bound (QCRB). However, the intrinsic incompatibility of quantum measurements represents a hurdle in extending these advantages to the simultaneous estimation of multiple parameters. In this paper, we present an experimental protocol approaching the QCRB simultaneously in two polarisation parameters, across a wide range of the parameter space, with as few as $\sim 200$ photon pairs, offering advantages for polarimetric sensing for dim sources such as in X-ray astronomy or photosensitive samples.

</details>


### [107] [I-QMapper: Error-Aware Layout Optimization and Device Diagnostics for NISQ Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27508)
*Milana Bazayeva,Kenneth Merz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍I-QMapper，一个基于Jupyter的开源交互式工具，用于超导量子硬件上的噪声感知布局选择、可视化和分析。该工具提供通用模式和专用量子化学模式（针对LUCJ ansatz），包含设计面板、带四种时间视图的误差分析面板、布局质量评分（LQS）、多编程支持、双后端并排比较及会话导出功能，旨在支持NISQ设备上快速布局原型设计和噪声感知实验设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子映射要么通过手动检查校准数据，要么通过自动布局流水线完成，两者都无法将交互式布局可视化与校准分析相结合，导致NISQ硬件上难以实现高保真执行。

Method: 开发I-QMapper：基于Jupyter的开源交互式工具，提供通用模式和量子化学专用模式（针对LUCJ ansatz）；设计面板支持交互式布局构建；误差面板提供四种时间视图（Live、Snapshot、Intraday、Multi-day）及阈值过滤和delta模式比较；定义布局质量评分（LQS）聚合读取和两量子比特门误差；支持多编程设置和双后端并排比较。

Result: 实现了集布局可视化、校准分析和噪声感知决策于一体的交互式工具，支持快速布局原型设计和实验重现。

Conclusion: I-QMapper通过将交互式探索与校准分析相结合，有助于在NISQ设备上实现信息充分的噪声感知实验设计和快速布局原型设计。

Abstract: Achieving high-fidelity execution on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) hardware requires careful selection of physical qubit layouts, as gate errors, readout errors, and coherence times vary across the device and drift over time. Currently, qubit mapping is performed either through manual inspection of device calibration data or through automated layout pipelines, neither of which provides integrated, interactive layout visualization combined with calibration analytics. In this work, we present the Interactive Quantum Mapper (I-QMapper), a Jupyter-based, open-source tool for noise-aware layout selection, visualization, and analysis on superconducting quantum hardware. I-QMapper offers two operating modes: a general-purpose mode for arbitrary circuits, and a dedicated mode for quantum-chemistry applications, specifically tailored to the Local Unitary Cluster Jastrow (LUCJ) ansatz. Within each mode, a Design panel supports interactive layout construction, while an Error panel provides calibration analytics through four temporal viewing modes (Live, Snapshot, Intraday, and Multi-day range) together with threshold filtering and delta-mode comparison for drift identification. Each layout receives a Layout-Quality Score (LQS) that aggregates the readout and two-qubit gate errors of the layout into a single quality value. Starting from the automatic LUCJ circuit-generation provided by IBM Quantum, we extend it to a multi-programming setting in which multiple circuits are mapped onto a single quantum processing unit (QPU). I-QMapper further supports side-by-side visualization of two quantum backends and layout comparison, and session export for experimental reproducibility. By combining interactive exploration with calibration analytics, I-QMapper aims to support both rapid layout prototyping and informed noise-aware experimental design on NISQ devices.

</details>


### [108] [Photon avalanche triggered by a single photon in a bistable nonlinear optical cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27555)
*Asian Selvakumaran,Anna Minguzzi,Iacopo Carusotto,Maxime Richard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了相干驱动的非线性光学腔对单个入射光子的响应，揭示了单光子可以触发从低光子数态到高光子数态的巨大量子跳跃，并基于光学双稳态的相变图景解释了这一现象。研究还提出了一种全光单光子雪崩探测器的可能策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非线性光学腔中量子涨落和非平衡动力学的角色，并研究单个光子能否引发系统的巨量响应，从而为全光单光子探测提供理论基础。

Method: 采用量子描述方法，全面考虑腔场的量子涨落和入射光子的离散性质，对非线性动力学进行理论分析，表征单光子刺激下从低光子数态到高光子数态的光学双稳态跳跃的量子动力学。

Result: 系统对单个量子激发可以表现出巨量响应，该响应根植于光学双稳态的相变图景。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅揭示了非线性光学腔非平衡动力学中量子涨落的作用，还提出了一种全光单光子雪崩探测器的设计策略。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the response of a coherently-driven nonlinear optical cavity to an additional incident single photon. Using a quantum description of the nonlinear dynamics that fully accounts for the quantum fluctuations of the cavity field and for the discrete nature of the incident photon, we characterize the quantum dynamics of single-photon-stimulated jumps from the low-photon-number to the high-photon-number state of the optical bistability loop. We find that the system can exhibit a giant response to this single quantum of excitation, rooted in the phase-transition picture of optical bistability. In addition to shedding light on the role of quantum fluctuations in the non-equilibrium dynamics of a nonlinear optical cavity, our results suggest a strategy for an all-optical single-photon avalanche detector.

</details>


### [109] [Vector Representation of Exact Soliton Dynamics in Multi-component Nonlinear Schrödinger Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27569)
*Maxime Foucher,Laurent Delisle,Amine Jaouadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对可积Manakov系统的矢量Hirota双线性形式，直接在矢量层面处理耦合非线性薛定谔方程，系统构建了亮、暗及混合单/双/三孤子解，为多组分非线性激发提供了统一描述框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分量分解方法处理多组分非线性薛定谔方程时丧失系统的内在矢量结构，需要一种能保留多组分表示本质的双线性化框架，以统一描述矢量孤子及其非线性相互作用。

Method: 发展矢量Hirota双线性形式，直接在矢量层面（而非分量分解）处理Manakov系统，在双线性化全程保留矢量系统的几何组织，利用该框架系统构造亮、暗及混合类型的1-3孤子解。

Result: 成功构造了亮、暗及混合的一阶、二阶和三阶矢量孤子解；证明矢量结构能统一描述不同组分间的非线性相互作用；所得解析表达式紧凑且显式体现组分间耦合。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅简化了解析处理，还为矢量Hirota方法推广至其他精确解（如怪波、周期波和有理解）奠定了基础，为多组分相干物质波系统的研究提供了自然视角。

Abstract: Multicomponent nonlinear Schrödinger equations constitute fundamental models for coherent matter waves in multicomponent Bose--Einstein condensates, spinor quantum fluids, and vector nonlinear optical systems. We develop a vector formulation of the Hirota bilinear formalism for the completely integrable Manakov system that treats the coupled nonlinear Schrödinger equations directly at the vector level rather than through the conventional component-wise decomposition. This framework naturally retains the intrinsic multicomponent representation of the model while providing compact analytical expressions for exact vector soliton solutions. Within this approach, we systematically construct bright, dark, and mixed one-, two-, and three-soliton solutions and show how the underlying vector structure provides a unified description of their nonlinear interactions. In particular, the proposed formalism makes the coupling between the different components explicit while preserving the geometric organization of the vector system throughout the bilinearization procedure. Beyond its analytical simplicity, the framework offers a natural perspective for the study of coherent multicomponent nonlinear excitations and provides a foundation for extending vector Hirota methods to other classes of exact solutions, including rogue waves, periodic waves, and rational solutions.

</details>


### [110] [su(1,1) Symmetry and Exact Solutions of the Dunkl-Klein-Gordon Equation in Higher Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27589)
*M. Salazar-Ramírez,B. C. Lütfüoğlu,Jesús Alfredo Martínez-Nuño*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在代数框架下研究d维Dunkl-Klein-Gordon方程，通过薛定谔分解构造su(1,1)代数生成元，推导能谱和Sturmian径向基，分析Dunkl-Klein-Gordon谐振子和类Coulomb势的束缚态，并构造SU(1,1)相干态，发现Dunkl变形引入宇称依赖的空间修正但保持代数动力学不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Dunkl变形引入相对论性Klein-Gordon方程，在d维空间中建立代数框架，探索Dunkl算子对量子系统可解性和对称性的影响。

Method: 采用薛定谔因子化方法构造su(1,1)代数生成元，利用不可约酉表示推导能谱，解析求解Sturmian径向基，通过SU(1,1)相干态分析时间演化。

Result: 获得了Dunkl-Klein-Gordon谐振子和Dunkl-Coulomb势束缚态的能谱与径向波函数；相干态表现出特征径向振荡行为；Dunkl变形导致宇称依赖的空间修正。

Conclusion: Dunkl变形在保持系统底层代数动力学结构的同时，引入了宇称依赖的空间结构修正，丰富了相对论性量子系统的可解模型。

Abstract: We investigate the $d$-dimensional Dunkl--Klein--Gordon equation for a scalar particle within an algebraic framework. By employing Schrödinger factorization, we construct the generators of the $\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$ algebra and establish the associated symmetry of the radial sector. The energy spectrum is derived using irreducible unitary representations, and the corresponding Sturmian radial basis is obtained analytically. We analyze the $d$-dimensional Dunkl--Klein--Gordon oscillator and the bound-state sector of the $d$-dimensional Dunkl--Klein--Gordon equation with a Dunkl--Coulomb-like potential. Furthermore, $\mathrm{SU}(1,1)$ coherent states are constructed and their time evolution is analyzed, revealing a characteristic radial oscillation behavior. The results show that the Dunkl deformation introduces parity-dependent modifications in the spatial structure of the system while preserving its underlying algebraic dynamics.

</details>


### [111] [Scalable Acceleration of Many-Body Quantum Dynamics via Time-Rescaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27630)
*Edson B. de Almeida Filho,Ângelo F. da Silva França,Bertúlio de Lima Bernardo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出时间重标定（TR）方法可有效加速封闭多体量子系统的动力学演化，在横场伊辛模型中显著提升量子退火性能，实现高保真度的GHZ态制备，并证明Mandelstam-Tamm量子速度极限不限制TR的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速量子控制对于克服当前量子平台中的退相干至关重要，但在多体系统中实现这一目标仍面临重大挑战。

Method: 采用时间重标定（TR）方法，将其应用于存在纵向场的横场伊辛模型，通过时间尺度的重新标定加速封闭多体量子系统的动力学演化。

Result: TR方法显著提升了量子退火性能，在标准绝热动力学失效的演化时间内保持高基态保真度，且对系统尺寸依赖性弱；实现了多体系统中GHZ态的高保真度制备，在固定演化时间内扩展了可访问的系统尺寸；揭示了Mandelstam-Tamm量子速度极限不限制TR加速能力，因为演化时间缩短被能量波动增加精确补偿。

Conclusion: TR方法是一种可扩展且实验可行的多体系统快速量子控制方案。

Abstract: Fast quantum control is essential to overcome decoherence in contemporary quantum platforms, yet achieving this in many-body systems remains a major challenge. We show that the time-rescaling (TR) method enables efficient acceleration of closed many-body quantum dynamics, extending its applicability beyond previously studied regimes. Applying TR to the transverse-field Ising model with a longitudinal field, we demonstrate a significant enhancement of quantum annealing performance, maintaining high ground-state fidelity at evolution times where standard adiabatic dynamics breaks down, with only weak dependence on system size. We further demonstrate high-fidelity preparation of Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger states in many-body systems, where TR extends the accessible system sizes within fixed evolution times. We additionally show that the Mandelstam-Tamm quantum speed limit does not fundamentally limit the acceleration achievable through TR, as the reduction in evolution time is exactly compensated by increased energy fluctuations. These results establish TR as a scalable and experimentally viable approach to fast quantum control in many-body systems.

</details>


### [112] [Enhancing Quantum Metrology with High-order Fisher Information and Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27633)
*Xin-Zhu Liu,Jun-Li Jiang,Yan-Han Yang,Li-Ming Zhao,Xue Yang,Shao-Ming Fei,Ming-Xing Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高阶Fisher信息的新信息度量，导出了参数估计的广义不确定关系（Cramér-Rao界的扩展），并在单比特量子相位估计中与已知层级界进行了比较，最后通过光子平台实验验证了该框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fisher信息在统计与量子计量学中至关重要，是Cramér-Rao界的基础。现有框架主要基于一阶Fisher信息，高阶信息尚未被充分探索用于构建新的不确定关系。本文旨在引入高阶Fisher信息度量，以推广参数估计中的不确定关系。

Method: 提出基于高阶Fisher信息的信息度量，推导广义不确定关系作为Cramér-Rao界的扩展；在单比特量子相位估计场景中与已知层级界进行理论比较；利用光子平台进行实验验证。

Result: 成功导出了广义不确定关系；在单比特相位估计中展示了新界与已有层级界的关系；光子平台实验数据支持了理论框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 高阶Fisher信息度量能够自然导出参数估计中的广义不确定关系，拓展了经典Cramér-Rao界，并在量子相位估计中通过实验得到了验证。

Abstract: Fisher information plays a central role in statistics and quantum metrology, providing the basis for the celebrated Cramér-Rao bound. In this work, we introduce a new information measure based on higher-order Fisher information and show that it naturally leads to a generalized uncertainty relation for parameter estimation, which can be regarded as an extension of the Cramér-Rao bound. As an application, we analyze the case of quantum phase estimation with a single qubit and compare our theoretical bounds with the well-known established hierarchical bounds. Finally, we experimentally validate the proposed framework using a photonic platform.

</details>


### [113] [Memory and thermal amplification in spin--cavity squared commutators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27648)
*Yong-Hong Ma,Hui-Hui Xu,Jian-Zhuang Wu,Quan-Zhen Ding,Wu-Ming Liu,Xin-Yu Zhao,E Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Holstein-Primakoff极限下研究了自旋-腔系统的平方对易子，提出了一种在有限温度含记忆 reservoirs 中区分传播与协方差增长的紧凑方法。通过有限温度NMQSD构造，线性正交对易子由延迟自旋-腔传播子固定，而二次对易子携带相同延迟因子及协方差因子。对于零均值高斯态，对称化表达式给出自旋边和混合通道。计算表明，热光子数仅影响协方差扇区而不影响均匀延迟核，改变 bath 记忆速率和反旋耦合在稳定HP区域内可调控传输权重及其时间分布。该方法实现了集体自旋-腔动力学中记忆依赖传播与热协方差增长的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自旋-腔系统动力学研究在有限温度和记忆效应下难以区分传播过程与协方差增长的贡献。本文旨在开发一种基于平方对易子的解析框架，在Holstein-Primakoff极限下实现两者的解耦分析。

Method: 采用有限温度非马尔可夫量子态扩散（NMQSD）构造，推导线性与二次对易子的解析表达式。通过引入平方对易子，将动力学分解为延迟传播子控制的传播部分和协方差函数控制的热涨落部分。在零均值高斯态假设下导出对称化表达式，并数值分析热光子数、bath记忆速率和反旋耦合等参数的影响。

Result: 线性正交对易子由延迟自旋-腔传播子唯一确定；二次对易子包含相同传播因子与协方差因子的乘积。热光子数从0增至1时线性传输不变但二次信号增强。bath记忆速率和反旋耦合在稳定HP区域内调控传输权重及其时间分布。

Conclusion: 平方对易子框架成功实现了有限温度记忆浴中自旋-腔系统传播与协方差增长的分离，为理解热效应下集体自旋动力学的记忆依赖传输机制提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Squared commutators in the Holstein--Primakoff limit of a spin--cavity system provide a compact way to separate propagation from covariance growth in a finite-temperature reservoir with memory. In the finite-temperature NMQSD construction, the linear quadrature commutator is fixed by the retarded spin--cavity propagator, whereas a quadratic commutator carries the same retarded factor together with a covariance factor. For a zero-mean Gaussian state, \(C_{R_i^2,R_j}(t)=4|κ_{ij}(t)|^2V_{ii}(t)\); the symmetrized expression gives the spin-side and mixed channels. Since \(\bar n\) enters the covariance sector but not the homogeneous retarded kernel, raising \(\bar n\) from 0 to 1 leaves the linear transfer unchanged while increasing the quadratic signal. Varying the bath-memory rate and the counter-rotating coupling within the stable HP region then shows how stored cavity history changes both the transfer weight and its distribution in time. The calculation separates memory-dependent propagation from thermal covariance growth in collective spin--cavity dynamics.

</details>


### [114] [On the generic structures of the protocols for quantum auction and quantum summation and their relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27693)
*Kabir Sandhu,Sandeep Mishra,Anirban Pathak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了量子拍卖与量子求和协议之间的结构对称性，证明了拍卖核心原语可约化为求和预言机的重复调用，反之亦然，从而将求和确立为拍卖机制的统一原语，并通过IBM量子硬件进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子拍卖和量子求和方案通常是独立发展的，缺乏统一的理论框架。本文旨在揭示二者之间的结构联系，建立统一的原语视角。

Method: 通过理论分析识别量子拍卖和量子求和协议中的结构对称性，将拍卖核心原语（收益估计、最高出价识别、胜出者确定）约化为对求和预言机的重复调用，同时将求和协议嵌入为拍卖框架中的子程序。在计算、通信和存储成本方面进行了对比分析，并在IBM量子硬件和光学量子平台上进行了数值验证。

Result: 建立了量子拍卖与量子求和之间的可约化关系，证明了求和是拍卖机制的统一原语；现有拍卖协议在实现求和任务时存在与出价空间探索和胜出者确定相关的额外开销；该框架与具体协议无关，适用于门模型和光子模型等多种计算模型；在两投标者密封投标拍卖中进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: 量子拍卖和量子求和之间存在本质的结构等价性，求和可作为统一原语服务于多种安全多方计算任务。该框架具有协议无关性，且实验验证了其实际可行性。

Abstract: Secure multi-party computation (SMC) addresses the problem of jointly computing global functions of private inputs while revealing minimal information about individual data. Two prominent examples of SMC tasks are sealed-bid auction and secure multi-party summation. Existing schemes for quantum auction and quantum summation have largely been developed independently, motivated by distinct applications and employing different computational primitives. In this work, structural symmetries in existing protocols for quantum auction and quantum summation are identified. In particular, it is established that the core auction primitives including revenue estimation, maximum bid identification, and winner determination can be reduced to repeated invocations of a summation oracle acting on suitably defined indicator functions. Conversely, summation protocols can be naturally embedded as auxiliary subroutines within auction frameworks, establishing summation as a unifying primitive underlying a broad class of auction mechanisms. Further, computational, communication and memory costs of these reductions are analyzed and compared with some of the representative existing protocols. The analysis has revealed that the process of implementing summation tasks through currently known auction protocols leads to additional overhead associated with bid-space exploration and winner determination. The proposed framework is protocol-agnostic and applicable across diverse computational models, including gate-based and photonic implementations. Finally, a proof-of-concept experimental realization (numerical validation) of a two-bidder sealed-bid auction using IBM (optical quantum) hardware is demonstrated to establish that the claimed equivalence is not merely formal but experimentally verifiable with the available hardware.

</details>


### [115] [Fault tolerant computation of the static structure factor and finite size effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27734)
*Rishabh Bhardwaj,Alexander Reed Muñoz,Travis E. Jones,John Golden*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于周期材料基态能量估计的量子后处理策略，通过构造Bloch轨道基下的结构因子算符并进行块编码，利用放大Hadamard测试测量红外密度涨落，从而实现对有限尺寸误差中主导的两体修正项的抑制，且计算开销低于主程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法在处理周期材料时面临有限尺寸误差问题——量子算法只能评估有限超胞或有限布里渊区网格，而材料性质定义在热力学极限下。现有的有限尺寸纠偏方法（如下采样）代价高昂，亟需一种高效的后处理方法。

Method: 1. 通过twist averaging抑制单体壳效应；2. 将主导残留误差归因于长波密度涨落，由小动量静态结构因子S(q)编码；3. 在Bloch轨道基下构造结构因子算符，通过密度算符实现块编码；4. 利用放大Hadamard测试估计基态期望值；5. 引入自适应全局与局部二分搜索确定红外拟合窗口，用于重建两体有限尺寸误差修正。

Result: 结构因子修正的渐近复杂度为Õ(N_b N_k)³（依赖Bloch轨道基大小），避免了全哈密顿量模拟的大平面波预因子，仅需Õ(N_b N_k)个逻辑量子比特，开销低于主基态能量估计程序。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种容错的替代下采样方案，将重复的大尺寸能量计算替换为对控制有限尺寸效应的红外密度关联的目标测量，有效解决了周期材料量子计算中的有限尺寸修正问题。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum algorithms offer a promising pathway for estimating the ground-state energies of periodic materials that are beyond the practical reach of classical electronic-structure methods. A remaining challenge is finite-size mitigation: quantum algorithms evaluate a finite supercell or finite Brillouin-zone mesh, while materials properties are defined in the thermodynamic limit. In this work we develop a quantum post-processing strategy for the leading two-body finite-size correction. After one-body shell effects are reduced by twist averaging, the dominant residual error is controlled by long-wavelength density fluctuations, which are encoded in the small-momentum static structure factor $S(q)$. We formulate the corresponding operator in a Bloch-orbital basis, construct its block encoding through the density operator, and estimate its ground-state expectation value using an amplified Hadamard test. We also introduce adaptive global and local binary search procedures for identifying the infrared fitting window used to reconstruct the two-body finite size error correction. The resulting cost remains subleading relative to the main ground-state energy estimation routine: the structure-factor correction has leading $\tilde{O}(N_bN_k)^3$ dependence on the Bloch-orbital basis size, avoids the large plane-wave prefactor of full Hamiltonian simulation, and requires only $\tilde{O}(N_bN_k)$ logical qubits. This provides a fault-tolerant alternative to down-sampling, replacing repeated energy calculations on larger cells with targeted measurements of the infrared density correlations that control the finite-size effects.

</details>


### [116] [No Cloning of Quantum Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27756)
*Zhenyu Du,Siyuan Cheng,Qi Zhao,Xiongfeng Ma,Xiao Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度证明了量子系综的不可克隆定理，并揭示了对有限时间演化生成的物理系综可意外规避该限制，但相关任务在计算上仍然是难解的，从而建立了量子力学中样本复杂度、计算复杂度和量子测量之间的基本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代量子物理已能在单个轨迹层面控制量子系统，揭示了许多非平衡现象（如深度热化和测量诱导纠缠），但这些现象的表征依赖于测量单个量子态的非线性性质——这本质上相当于对量子系综进行精细克隆。因此，需要从基本原理层面理解量子系综克隆的可行性及局限性。

Method: 从信息论视角出发，建立了任意量子系综的普遍不可克隆定理，即使假设拥有系综纯化态的多个副本也不可克隆。随后证明，对于由有限时间演化生成的物理系综，这一障碍可以被意外规避。然而，进一步证明即使已知态制备的完整电路描述，这些任务在计算上仍然是难解的。

Result: 建立了量子系综的不可克隆定理；发现有限时间演化生成的物理系综可规避该定理；但相关任务的经典或量子计算复杂度仍然是难解的，与依赖"态未知"的传统不可克隆定理形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 这些结果建立了量子力学的新基本原理，揭示了样本复杂度、计算复杂度和量子测量之间的内在权衡，并强调了探测测量诱导量子现象需要针对具体问题设计策略。

Abstract: Modern quantum physics now enables control of quantum systems at the level of individual trajectories, opening a new frontier that links quantum information theory, quantum many-body physics, and quantum thermodynamics, and uncovers novel non-equilibrium phenomena such as deep thermalization and measurement-induced entanglement. However, a central challenge remains: their characterization relies on measuring nonlinear properties of individual quantum states, a task tantamount to fine-grained cloning of a quantum ensemble. Here, the fundamental laws governing the cloning of quantum ensembles are investigated. First, a general no-cloning theorem for arbitrary ensembles is established from an information-theoretic perspective, even assuming multiple copies of the ensemble's purification. It is then shown that this barrier can be unexpectedly circumvented for physical ensembles generated by finite-time evolutions. Nevertheless, these tasks are proven to remain computationally intractable, even when the full circuit description of state preparation is known. This stands in sharp contrast to the conventional no-cloning theorem, which relies on the state being unknown. Together, these results establish new fundamental principles of quantum mechanics, reveal intrinsic trade-offs among sample complexity, computational complexity, and quantum measurements, and highlight the necessity of problem-specific strategies for probing measurement-induced quantum phenomena.

</details>


### [117] [Industry-ready spin-photon interfaces for hybrid photonic quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27787)
*Hêlio Huet,Hubert Lam,Thibaut Pollet,Petr Steindl,Alice Bernard,Albert Adiyatullin,Petr Stepanov,William Hease,Victor Guilloux,Nico Margaria,Joris Verstraten,Raksha Singla,Samuel T. Mister,Anton Pishchagin,Lara Couronné,Samuel Huber,David Sebastian,Duc Duy Tran,Thi Hao Nhi Nguyen,Thi Phuong Do,Joseph Sulpizio,Yann Portella,Kiarn T. Laverick,Thinhinane Bennour,Tomas Alexandre De Sousa,Davide Stefani,Mathias Pont,Maxime Descampeaux,Bianca Scaparra,Martin A. Jacobsen,Klaus D. Jöns,Rinaldo Trotta,Aristide Lemaître,Martina Morassi,Olivier Krebs,Loïc Lanco,Niels Gregersen,Alexia Auffèves,Maria Maffei,Shane Mansfield,Jean Senellart,Thomas Volz,Viviana Villafañe,Stephen C. Wein,Dario A. Fioretto,Sebastien Boissier,Thi Huong Au,Pascale Senellart*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用III-V族半导体量子点微腔器件，通过工业级制造工艺实现高性能单光子源、自旋-多光子纠缠及远距离光子不可区分性，为混合光子量子计算提供了可扩展平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合光子量子计算机需要高性能、可重复、可扩展的腔耦合物质量子比特作为光-物质界面，但现有半导体量子点器件在性能、可重复性和自旋相干性方面尚未达到大规模纠错所需的阈值。

Method: 采用III-V族半导体量子点单光子源，通过工业级前道量产线工艺制造数千个器件；利用正交场量子态重构联合检验效率与不可区分性；在低磁场条件下实现七体自旋-多光子纠缠。

Result: 实现了近单位光子量子纯度（稳定数十分钟）、创纪录的单光子Wigner函数负值、七体自旋-多光子纠缠、微秒级自旋相干时间，以及远距离源光子与单源连续发射光子同等不可区分性。

Conclusion: 证明与代工厂兼容的III-V族量子点是实现大规模混合光子量子计算的可扩展平台，其性能指标支持容错阈值以下的光学损耗路径。

Abstract: Hybrid photonic quantum computers, combining stationary matter qubits and flying photonic qubits, offer an intrinsically networked and resource-efficient route to large-scale, error-corrected quantum computation. Their core components are cavity-coupled matter qubits that act as light--matter interfaces, enabling: high-efficiency on-demand single-photon generation, stable near-unity photon indistinguishability and spin--multi-photon entanglement. Semiconductor quantum dots in microcavities are a leading platform for realizing such devices. Yet reaching the performance, reproducibility and spin-coherence thresholds for large-scale error correction remains a major challenge requiring industrial fabrication and control. Here we report thousands of monolithic semiconductor quantum-dot devices fabricated using a III--V pilot production-line process compatible with large-scale deployment. Systematic control of source parameters yields state-of-the-art efficiency and supports a path to optical losses below fault-tolerance thresholds. Using field-quadrature state reconstruction as a stringent joint test of efficiency and indistinguishability, we observe near-unity photon quantum purity stable over tens of minutes and a record single-photon Wigner-function negativity. We further demonstrate seven-partite spin--multi-photon entanglement and spin coherence extendable to microsecond timescales in the low-magnetic-field regime. Finally, photons from distant sources are as indistinguishable as photons emitted successively by a single source. These results establish foundry-compatible III--V quantum dots as a scalable platform for hybrid photonic quantum computing.

</details>


### [118] [Single-sideband-interference twin-field quantum key distribution without global phase locking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27792)
*Xingjian Li,Bingkun Wang,Jianyong Hu,Jianqiang Liu,Shuxiao Wu,Guosheng Feng,Zhixing Qiao,Changgang Yang,Ruiyun Chen,Chengbing Qin,Guofeng Zhang,Liantuan Xiao,Suotang Jia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并演示了一种无需全局锁相的单边带干涉TF-QKD协议。通过利用IQ调制器产生弱单边带作为量子信号，其相位关联的光载波作为实时相位参考，将相干时间要求从微秒级降低到纳秒级。实验在100.8 km光纤上实现了98%的干涉可见度，并在高损耗区域密钥率超越了PLOB界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TF-QKD虽能突破无中继量子链路的速率损耗极限，但其实际部署长期受限于两个独立激光器之间需要全局锁相的要求，这大大增加了系统复杂度和实现难度。

Method: 重新审视光学干涉基本原理，发现TF-QKD的干涉仅依赖于两独立光脉冲在时间重叠瞬时的相位对齐，而非连续全局相位同步。基于此洞察，提出单边带干涉TF-QKD方案：每个用户使用IQ调制器生成弱单边带作为量子信号，相位关联的光载波作为实时相位参考；接收端通过载波干扰实现实时相位提取和反馈补偿。

Result: 在100.8 km光纤上实现98%的干涉可见度，密钥率在高损耗区域超越PLOB界，相干时间要求从微秒/秒级降低到纳秒级。

Conclusion: 该方案消除了全局锁相的需求，大幅降低了TF-QKD的工程实现难度，为实用化长距离量子通信网络提供了一条更简捷的路径。

Abstract: Twin-field quantum key distribution (TF QKD) can overcome the fundamental rate loss limit of repeaterless quantum links, but its practical deployment has long been hindered by the requirement of global phase locking between two independent lasers. By revisiting the fundamental principles of optical interference, this work reveals that interference in TF QKD inherently relies only on the instantaneous phase alignment of two independent optical pulses at the moment they temporally overlap, rather than on continuous global phase synchronization. Guided by this insight, we propose and demonstrate a single-sideband-interference TF-QKD protocol that eliminates global phase locking. Each user employs an IQ modulator to generate a weak single sideband as the quantum signal, while the intrinsically phase-correlated optical carrier propagates as a real-time phase reference. Carrier interference at the receiver enables real-time phase extraction and feedback compensation for the sidebands. Unlike prior no phase locking approaches requiring second- or microsecond-level coherence, in principle, our scheme reduces this requirement to nanoseconds. We achieve 98% interference visibility over 100.8 km fibre and secure key rates surpassing the PLOB bound in the high-loss regime, providing a simpler route towards practical long-distance quantum communication networks.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum Dynamic Time Warping for Multivariate Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27815)
*Diego Alvarez-Estevez,Alejandro Mayorga-Redondo,Eduardo Mosqueira-Rey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合量子动态时间规整(qDTW)架构，用量子希尔伯特空间的参数化几何替代经典欧氏距离度量。通过引入统一预嵌入伴随拟设(Unified Pre-Embedding Adjoint Ansatz)解耦可训练纠缠与经典数据，解决了传统测量中的相位混淆和信息瓶颈问题。发现了严格的空间-时间表达性权衡：时间深度对于维度受限的单变量电路是必要的，但在宽多量子比特寄存器上会导致混沌频谱爆炸和表示坍缩。该多元量子架构在基准测试中超越了经典基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典DTW依赖欧氏距离，无法捕捉复杂多变量数据中潜在跨通道相关性，需要引入量子计算的参数化几何来提升序列对齐能力。

Method: 提出qDTW混合架构，用量子希尔伯特空间参数化度量替代经典距离；设计Unified Pre-Embedding Adjoint Ansatz解耦纠缠与数据；通过结构消融实验建立量子序列对齐拓扑规则。

Result: 在C≤8空间维度的基准测试中，未训练的量子核可作为高表达性基线，参数化训练能有效解开深度重叠的超高维数据。发现了空间-时间表达性权衡规律。

Conclusion: 多元量子架构通过规避拓扑风险，在动态规划中集成参数化量子电路，超越了经典基线，为量子序列对齐设立了新标准。

Abstract: Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) is a cornerstone for time series classification, but its reliance on Euclidean distances fails to capture latent cross-channel correlations in complex multivariate data. We propose a hybrid Quantum Dynamic Time Warping (qDTW) architecture, replacing the classical distance metric with the parameterized geometry of a quantum Hilbert space. Through structural ablation on benchmarks up to $C=8$ spatial dimensions, we establish fundamental topological rules for quantum sequence alignment.
  We introduce a Unified Pre-Embedding Adjoint Ansatz that decouples trainable entanglement from classical data, eliminating the severe phase-scrambling and information bottlenecks inherent to traditional measurements. We demonstrate this decoupled architecture allows untrained quantum kernels to act as highly expressive baselines, while parameterized training effectively untangles deeply overlapping hyper-dimensional data.
  Furthermore, we identify a strict spatial-temporal expressivity tradeoff: temporal depth (data re-uploading) is necessary for dimensionally restricted univariate circuits, but applying it to wide multi-qubit registers triggers chaotic frequency-spectrum explosions and representation collapse. By navigating these topological hazards, our multivariate quantum architecture outperforms classical baselines, setting a new standard for integrating parameterized quantum circuits with dynamic programming

</details>


### [120] [Detector-Conditioned Source-Space Nulls and Null-Mask Loss in a Programmable Two-Slit Interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27817)
*Jianming Wen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出并分析了时间反演的Young-Afshar干涉配置，将Afshar实验中的零检验从下游场平面转移到源标签平面。在该互易几何中，可寻址点光源照明双缝，探测器固定，通过扫描源坐标重建探测器条件响应。源平面掩模在双缝同时打开时对该探测器不可见，单缝打开时可见。建立了标量菲涅尔模型，推导了源空间零条件，引入探测器条件零掩模损失，并验证了互补性关系未被违反，而是呈现出互易的、探测器条件化的互补性形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Afshar双缝实验的时间反演对应构型，重新审视波粒互补性在互易几何中的表现形式，将零检验从场平面转移到源平面。

Method: 提出时间反演Young-Afshar配置，采用点可寻址光源和固定探测器，通过扫描源坐标重建干涉条纹；建立标量菲涅尔模型，推导源空间零条件，引入探测器条件零掩模损失，分析其在可调路径标记下的演化。

Result: 源空间可见度和路径可区分性满足标准对偶关系，互补性未被违反；Afshar的场空间透明性被源空间响应函数透明性替代。

Conclusion: 该配置不违反互补性原理，而是展现了一种互易的、探测器条件化的互补性形式，核心新特征是响应函数透明性替代了Afshar的场空间透明性。

Abstract: Afshar's double-slit experiment probes wave--particle complementarity by placing a wire grid at the dark fringes of a downstream interference pattern while retaining an imaging basis that appears to preserve which-path information. Here we propose and analyze a time-reversed Young--Afshar configuration in which the corresponding null test is transferred from the downstream field plane to the source-label plane of a time-reversed Young interferometer. In this reciprocal geometry, a point-addressable source illuminates a double slit, while the detector remains fixed. The observed fringe is therefore not a single-shot spatial intensity pattern, but a detector-conditioned response reconstructed by scanning the source coordinate. Consequently, a null in this pattern is not a node of a freely propagating field; it is a source label for which the coherent two-slit transfer amplitude to the selected detector vanishes. A mask placed at such source-plane labels is invisible to that detector when both slits are open, yet becomes visible when either slit is opened alone. We develop the scalar Fresnel model, derive the source-space null condition, introduce a detector-conditioned null-mask loss, and examine how this loss evolves under a tunable which-path marker. The source-space visibility and path distinguishability satisfy the standard duality relation, so no violation of complementarity is implied. The essential new feature is instead a reciprocal, detector-conditioned form of complementarity: Afshar's field-space transparency is replaced by response-function transparency in a reconstructed source basis.

</details>


### [121] [Parameter-Efficient Quantum-Inspired Fast Weight Programmers for Traffic-Matrix Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27821)
*Kuo-Chung Peng,Jiun-Cheng Jiang,Chun-Hua Lin,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于量子启发式紧致循环模型（QKAN-FWPs）在有限资源约束下进行网络流量矩阵多步预测的性能，发现G-QKANFWP在仅使用大LSTM 22.4%参数的情况下取得了最佳RMSE，并优于匹配规模的LSTM和经典G-FWP基线，为资源受限的网络流量矩阵预测提供了高精度-高效率的设计方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络在线控制中，流量矩阵（TM）预测面临内存、更新和训练预算等资源约束，现有方法依赖专用的图、Transformer或扩散模块，难以在轻量化条件下实现高精度全矩阵预测。本文旨在探索是否能用紧凑的量子启发式循环模型在资源受限环境下有效完成TM预测。

Method: 将门控量子启发式Kolmogorov-Arnold网络快速权重编程器（QKAN-FWPs）适配到Abilene TM数据集的多步预测任务中，预测未来20个五分钟帧（共144通道OD矩阵）。设计了三种QKAN placement变体，并与匹配规模的LSTM、更大规模LSTM以及经典门控快速权重编程器（G-FWP）在共享固定预算训练协议下进行对比。

Result: G-QKANFWP取得了最优的池化均方根误差（RMSE），仅使用大LSTM 22.4%的参数；同时优于匹配规模的LSTM和经典G-FWP基线。量子启发式变体在验证损失曲线下面积（AULC）指标上低于匹配规模的循环基线，G-QKANFWP和GQKAN-FWP在OD通道胜率上表现显著更优。

Conclusion: 经典慢编程器结合量子启发式快编程器（G-QKANFWP）是一种在资源受限网络流量矩阵预测中兼顾精度与效率的有前景的设计方案，性能提升并非仅来自门控快速权重框架本身。

Abstract: Traffic matrices (TMs) capture network-wide origin-destination demand and are central to traffic engineering, yet accurate whole-matrix forecasting remains challenging when prediction must be performed under the memory, update, and training-budget constraints of online network control. This paper investigates whether compact quantum-inspired recurrent models can provide effective TM forecasts without relying on dedicated graph, transformer, or diffusion modules. We adapt gated quantum-inspired Kolmogorov-Arnold network fast-weight programmers (QKAN-FWPs) to direct multi-step Abilene TM forecasting, where each model predicts the next 20 five-minute frames of a 144-channel origin-destination (OD) matrix from a two-hour history. We benchmark three QKAN placement variants against a matched-size long short-term memory (LSTM) network, a larger LSTM, and a classical gated fast-weight programmer under a shared fixed-budget training protocol. Among the evaluated recurrent models, G-QKANFWP achieves the best pooled root-mean-square error (RMSE), while using only 22.4% of the larger LSTM. It also outperforms both the matched-size LSTM and the classical G-FWP baseline, indicating that the gain is not due to gated fast-weight framework alone. Convergence and channel-wise analyses further show that the quantum-inspired variants obtain lower validation-loss area under the learning curve (AULC) than matched-size recurrent baselines, while G-QKANFWP and GQKAN-FWP achieve substantially more OD-channel wins. These results identify a classical slow programmer with a quantum-inspired fast programmer as a promising accuracy-efficiency design for resource-conscious network traffic-matrix forecasting.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum LiDAR with non-local modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27838)
*Xiao-Dong Fan,Zhong-Hua Ou,Yun-Ru Fan,Kai Guo,Zong-Liang Xie,Qiang Qi,Li-Xun Zhang,Si Shen,Hai-Zhi Song,Yan-Yu Wei,Hao Li,Li-Xing You,Qi Zhang,Yong Liu,Guang-Can Guo,Qiang Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种量子振幅调制连续波LiDAR系统，通过非局域调制和量子关联，在2-8米测量范围内实现了0.64±0.06毫米/秒的精度，500秒累积后可达29±4微米精度，并在37dB强噪声背景下相比经典单光子方案提升50倍精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子LiDAR系统难以同时实现高精度和大测量范围，亟需一种能兼顾二者的技术方案。

Method: 采用量子振幅调制连续波LiDAR架构，信号光子直接照射目标，闲散光子经高频余弦波非局域调制后不接触目标，利用非局域调制和量子关联实现目标检测。

Result: 在2-8米范围内达到0.64±0.06 mm/s精度；500秒累积后精度达29±4 μm；在37dB强噪声下精度较经典单光子方案提升50倍。

Conclusion: 该方法为发展高精度、远距离、抗噪声的目标检测技术开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum light detection and ranging (LiDAR) utilizes quantum entanglement and correlation to improve precision, noise resilience and covertness of target detection. Despite recent advances, the development of a quantum LiDAR system that simultaneously achieves high precision and a large measurement range remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate a quantum amplitude-modulated continuous wave LiDAR with micrometer precision achievable via increased acquisition time and meter-scale measurement range. In our demonstration, the signal photons directly illuminate the target, while the idler photons are non-locally modulated with a high-frequency cosine wave and never interact with the target. By leveraging the non-local modulation and the quantum correlation, the target detection is achieved with a precision of 0.64 $\pm$ 0.06 mm within one second over a measurement range of 2-8 m. As the acquisition time is up to 500 s, the system achieves a precision of 29 $\pm\ 4{\ \mathrm{μm}}$. Furthermore, our system realizes a 50 times precision improvement over the classical single-photon scheme in a background noise 37 dB stronger than the returned probe photons. With these advantages, our method will open venues for the development of high-precision, long-range, and noise-resilient target detection.

</details>


### [123] [Simulating the Dynamics of Markovian Quantum Processes by Quantum Collision Models on Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27856)
*Zeqing Wang,Julian D. Teske,Anshuman Bhardwaj,Masahiro O. Takahashi,Seiji Yunoki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在离子阱和超导量子计算机上实验模拟了具有局域和非局域耗散的马尔可夫量子过程，采用硬件特定辅助策略，实现了最多7个系统量子比特（总计13个量子比特）和40个时间步长的模拟，并表明最优实现策略强烈依赖于量子计算机的硬件特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子碰撞模型是实现量子计算机上非酉演化的自然方法，但以往实现受限于噪声、电路深度、辅助重置开销和量子比特资源，通常仅局限于1-2个系统量子比特和少于12个时间步长，亟需更大规模和更长时间步长的实验演示。

Method: 采用量子碰撞模型，通过将系统耦合到辅助量子比特来实现耗散；针对离子阱和超导两种量子计算平台，分别设计硬件特定的辅助量子比特策略，实现局域和非局域耗散的马尔可夫动力学模拟。

Result: 成功在两种量子计算平台上实现了最多7个系统量子比特（总计13个量子比特）和40个时间步长的马尔可夫量子过程模拟，远超此前实验规模。

Conclusion: 即使针对相同的物理模型，最优的实现策略也强烈依赖于量子计算机的硬件特性，表明硬件感知的算法设计对于近期量子计算实验至关重要。

Abstract: Hamiltonian dynamics have been widely implemented on noisy intermediate-scale quantum devices in recent years. In contrast, experimental demonstrations of Markovian quantum dynamics remain limited, because implementing nonunitary evolution on quantum computers is challenging. Quantum collision models provide a natural approach to this problem by coupling the system to ancillas to realize dissipation. However, previous implementations of quantum collision models on quantum computers have typically been restricted to one or two system qubits and fewer than 12 time steps, owing to noise, circuit depth, the overhead of ancilla reset, and limited qubit resources. In this work, we experimentally simulate Markovian quantum processes with local and nonlocal dissipation on both trapped-ion and superconducting quantum computers. By employing hardware-specific ancilla strategies, we realize simulations with up to seven system qubits, corresponding to 13 qubits in total, and 40 time steps. Our results demonstrate that, even for the same physical model, the optimal implementation strategy depends strongly on the hardware characteristics of the quantum computer.

</details>


### [124] [Standard-quantum-limit-surpassing vector polarimetry using Rydberg atoms in an SU(1,1) interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27870)
*Weiqiang Guan,Yuetao Chen,Jun Zhou,Keyi Li,Yu Huang,Shaoyan Gao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于里德伯原子的高灵敏度射频电场矢量偏振测量理论框架，通过将里德伯原子系统集成到SU(1,1)干涉仪中，利用同差检测提取原子吸收指数来确定极化角度，灵敏度在宽角度范围内超越标准量子极限，最佳灵敏度可达10⁻⁶度以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要集中于开发新型测量方案，但高灵敏度矢量偏振测量仍是一个悬而未决的问题，特别是弱场探测场景下的高精度偏振方向检测。

Method: 在静磁场下，利用里德伯原子塞曼子能级与射频场偏振分量之间耦合的不对称性，通过将里德伯原子系统集成到SU(1,1)干涉仪中，采用同差检测获取原子吸收指数，从而反演极化角度。推导了极化角度的灵敏度、标准量子极限和量子Cramér-Rao界。

Result: 使用双相干态或相干态与压缩真空态作为输入时，灵敏度在宽角度范围内超越标准量子极限；最佳灵敏度可达10⁻⁶度以下，且在大部分角度域内保持优于10⁻³度的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该工作为高精度矢量偏振测量建立了基础，推动了基于里德伯原子的量子传感发展，并加深了对光-物质相互作用的理解。

Abstract: Vector polarimetry is an important application frontier for Rydberg-atom-based sensing. While prior research has largely concentrated on developing novel measurement schemes, high-sensitivity vector polarimetry remains an open question. Here we propose a theoretical framework for high-sensitivity detection of radio-frequency (RF) electric field polarization direction, which is particularly suitable for weak-field detection. Under a static magnetic field, the asymmetry in coupling between the Zeeman sublevels of the Rydberg atom and the RF field's polarization components enables the polarization angles to be determined from the atomic absorption index, which is retrieved via homodyne detection by incorporating the Rydberg atom system into an SU(1,1) interferometer. We derive the sensitivity of the polarization angles along with the corresponding standard quantum limit (SQL) and quantum Cramér--Rao bound (QCRB). Our results demonstrate a sensitivity surpassing the SQL across wide angular ranges using either dual coherent states or a coherent state combined with a squeezed vacuum state as input. Significantly, the optimal sensitivity reaches below \SI{e-6}{\degree}, with sensitivities better than \SI{e-3}{\degree} maintained over most of the angular domain. This work establishes a foundation for high-precision vector polarimetry, thereby advancing the development of Rydberg-atom-based quantum sensing and contributing to a deeper understanding of light--matter interactions.

</details>


### [125] [Non-equilibrium quantum thermometry with bosonic samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27894)
*Marek Winczewski,Michał Horodecki,Ricard Ravell Rodríguez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用玻色子探针（量子谐振子）通过Drude-Ohmic谱密度与玻色子热库强耦合的非平衡低温量子测温学。通过精确解和重整化GKLS主方程分析，发现强耦合非马尔可夫区域中量子Fisher信息（QFI）随时间非单调演化，存在有限最优测量时间；而马尔可夫区域QFI单调递增至稳态，最优时间趋于无穷。压缩初态提供瞬时优势，但热化会消除该优势。强耦合在低温下将指数Boltzmann抑制替换为多项式发散。该模型可直接映射到电路QED实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强耦合、非马尔可夫效应对量子测温精度的影响，特别是低温非平衡条件下超出马尔可夫近似的新效应，以及压缩态探针在测温中的潜力。

Method: 采用Boyanovsky-Jasnow精确二次型解（精确处理探针-浴动力学）和重整化GKLS主方程（马尔可夫近似）两种方法，从含时协方差矩阵提取单模高斯探针态（包括压缩态）的量子Fisher信息。

Result: (1) 强耦合非马尔可夫区域：QFI呈非单调演化，出现浴记忆恢复现象，存在有限最优测量时间t*>0；(2) 马尔可夫区域：QFI单调递增至稳态，无内部最优，最优时间始终趋于无穷；(3) 压缩初态提供瞬态优势，但被热化消除；(4) 平衡态下，强耦合将低温相对误差的指数Boltzmann抑制替换为多项式发散。

Conclusion: 压缩和测量时间是互补的测温资源；强耦合可突破低温下的经典精度极限；该方案在当前电路QED实验条件下可行。

Abstract: We study low-temperature non-equilibrium quantum thermometry with a bosonic probe: a quantum harmonic oscillator strongly coupled to a bosonic bath at temperature $T$ through a Drude--Ohmic spectral density. We treat the probe--bath dynamics both exactly, using the quadratic solution of Boyanovsky and Jasnow, and within a renormalized Gorini--Kossakowski--Lindblad--Sudarshan (GKLS) master equation. From the time-dependent covariance matrix we extract the quantum Fisher information (QFI) for general single-mode Gaussian probe states, including squeezed ones. In the strong-coupling, non-Markovian regime the QFI is non-monotonic in time, displaying bath-memory revivals that make a finite interrogation time $t^*>0$ strictly optimal. By contrast, we prove that the Markovian QFI rises monotonically to its stationary value and develops no interior optimum, so that its optimum is always pinned to the boundary $t^*\to\infty$; this complements existing Markovian precision-rate bounds, which concern $(\mathcal F(t)/t)$ rather than the single-shot QFI $(\mathcal F(t))$. Squeezed initial states yield a large transient advantage that thermalisation eventually erases, establishing squeezing and interrogation time as complementary thermometric resources. At equilibrium, strong coupling replaces the exponential Boltzmann suppression of the low-temperature relative error by a far milder polynomial divergence. As the model maps directly onto circuit quantum electrodynamics, these protocols appear within current experimental reach.

</details>


### [126] [End-to-End Learning of Quantum Control on Latent Dynamical Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27907)
*Jun-Dong Zhong,Zong-Yuan Ge,Feng-Hua Ren,Zhao-Ming Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于长短期记忆网络的端到端量子控制框架，在低维潜流形中联合学习系统动力学和控制策略，实现从初始状态和环境参数到动力学轨迹和优化控制脉冲的单次前向映射。在含噪二能级系统的绝热加速和一维自旋链的状态转移任务中，保真度提升且优化成本降低三个数量级，并展现了对多参数时变噪声、不同初始态和驱动场的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子控制采用"模拟-再优化"的迭代范式，动力学模拟与控制设计解耦，导致计算开销大且可扩展性差，尤其在噪声环境下问题更为突出。

Method: 提出基于长短期记忆（LSTM）的端到端量子控制框架，在低维潜流形中联合学习系统动力学与控制策略，实现初始状态和环境参数到动力学轨迹和优化控制脉冲的单次前向映射。

Result: 在含噪二能级系统的绝热加速和一维自旋链的状态转移任务中，实现了精确的动力学预测与控制优化，保真度提升，优化成本相比传统迭代方法降低三个数量级，并展现出对多参数时变噪声、不同初始态和驱动场的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作引入了一种基于潜流形学习的数据驱动控制范式，消除了迭代优化的计算瓶颈，实现了复杂开放量子系统的实时自适应控制。

Abstract: Traditional quantum control relies on an iterative "simulate-then-optimize" paradigm, where dynamics simulation and control design are decoupled, leading to substantial computational overhead and limited scalability, particularly in noisy environments. Here, we propose an end-to-end quantum control framework based on long short-term memory, in which system dynamics and control strategies are learned jointly in a low dimensional latent manifold. The model directly maps initial states and environmental parameters to both dynamical trajectories and optimized control pulse in a single forward pass. The framework is validated on adiabatic speedup in a two-level system and state transfer in a one-dimensional spin chain under noise, achieving accurate dynamical prediction and control optimization. It improves the fidelity for both tasks and significantly reduces the optimization cost by three orders of magnitude compared with conventional iterative methods, while exhibiting strong generalization to multi-parameter, time-varying noise, as well as to different initial states and driving fields. Our work introduces a data-driven control paradigm based on latent manifold learning, reducing the computational bottleneck of iterative optimization and enabling real-time adaptive control of complex open quantum systems.

</details>


### [127] [Verifiable and Collusion-Resistant Multi-Party Quantum Private Set Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27994)
*Zixian Gong,Kun Tian,Yi Zhang,Fengxia Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于旋转量子构造的显式基数测试量子多方阈值集合求交协议，通过隐藏标签测量向量和OLE内积过程实现阈值判定，并在IBM Qiskit平台上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子TPSI协议通常依赖第三方解读最终结果，偏离了TPSI的基数测试范式，亟需一种具备显式基数测试能力的量子TPSI方案。

Method: 采用基于旋转的量子构造，单光子序列依次经过参与者侧数据旋转、TP-参与者掩码旋转和相关聚合旋转，生成隐藏标签测量向量；TP可完成最终测量但无法解读语义；通过基于OLE的内积过程和轻量级乱码电路实现阈值决策，仅在满足阈值条件时重建交集。

Result: 证明了协议的正确性和安全性，并通过IBM Qiskit平台上的量子电路仿真验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 本文成功实现了具备显式基数测试能力的量子多方TPSI协议，解决了现有协议依赖第三方解读结果的范式偏差问题。

Abstract: Threshold private set intersection (TPSI) allows parties to reveal their intersection only when its cardinality reaches a prescribed threshold. Existing quantum TPSI protocols typically rely on a third party (TP) to interpret the final results, which deviates from the cardinality-testing paradigm of TPSI. In this paper, we propose a quantum multiparty TPSI protocol with explicit cardinality testing. Our protocol develops a rotation-based quantum construction in which single-photon sequences are sequentially processed through participant-side data rotations, TP--participant masking rotations, and correlated aggregate rotations. This design produces hidden-label measurement vectors: TP can complete the final measurement, but cannot interpret the semantic meaning of the outcomes. Based on these hidden measurements, we further realize the threshold decision through an oblivious linear evaluation (OLE)-based inner product procedure and a lightweight garbled circuit, revealing only \(\mathbf 1[|\bigcap_i X_i|\ge τ]\) before conditional intersection reconstruction. We prove the correctness and security of the proposed protocol, and further validate its feasibility through quantum-circuit simulations implemented on the IBM \textsf{Qiskit} platform.

</details>

*Zixian Gong,Kun Tian,Yi Zhang,Fengxia Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于旋转量子构造的多方门限隐私集合交集（TPSI）协议，通过隐藏标签测量向量和OLE内积实现显式的基数测试，解决了现有量子TPSI依赖第三方解读结果的范式偏离问题，并在IBM Qiskit平台上验证了可行性。

  <summary>Details</summary>
