Result: 证明了协议的正确性和安全性，并通过IBM Qiskit平台的量子电路仿真验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 该协议在量子TPSI领域首次实现了不依赖第三方语义解读的显式基数测试，为量子安全多方计算提供了新范式。

Abstract: Threshold private set intersection (TPSI) allows parties to reveal their intersection only when its cardinality reaches a prescribed threshold. Existing quantum TPSI protocols typically rely on a third party (TP) to interpret the final results, which deviates from the cardinality-testing paradigm of TPSI. In this paper, we propose a quantum multiparty TPSI protocol with explicit cardinality testing. Our protocol develops a rotation-based quantum construction in which single-photon sequences are sequentially processed through participant-side data rotations, TP--participant masking rotations, and correlated aggregate rotations. This design produces hidden-label measurement vectors: TP can complete the final measurement, but cannot interpret the semantic meaning of the outcomes. Based on these hidden measurements, we further realize the threshold decision through an oblivious linear evaluation (OLE)-based inner product procedure and a lightweight garbled circuit, revealing only \(\mathbf 1[|\bigcap_i X_i|\ge τ]\) before conditional intersection reconstruction. We prove the correctness and security of the proposed protocol, and further validate its feasibility through quantum-circuit simulations implemented on the IBM \textsf{Qiskit} platform.

</details>


### [129] [Hard-core Bosons in Action: Applications to Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28004)
*David Emmanuel-Costa,Michael Epping*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了基于硬核玻色子代数的量子电路仿真框架，相比IBM Qiskit显著提升了仿真速度，并首次将该形式化方法与遗传算法结合用于量子电路综合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于复Clifford代数的量子电路表示方法需要符号修正，计算效率受限；硬核玻色子能直接实现多量子比特的张量积结构，无需符号修正，但此前缺乏高效的实现和更广泛的应用验证。

Method: 回顾并扩展了硬核玻色子代数用于电路仿真的理论基础，实现了高效的仿真器；将其与遗传算法结合，用于量子电路的综合与优化。

Result: 与IBM Qiskit的性能对比表明，硬核玻色子方法在仿真执行时间上显著优于Qiskit。

Conclusion: 硬核玻色子代数不仅在计算效率上优于传统Clifford代数方法，还可拓展至量子电路综合等新应用领域。

Abstract: The use of algebraic frameworks based on complex Clifford algebras for the representation and simulation of quantum circuits has been discussed in the literature. Recently, an alternative algebraic approach employing hard-core bosons has been proposed. Hard-core bosons provide a natural representation of multi-qubit systems, in which the tensor-product structure is realized directly and no sign corrections are required, in contrast to realizations based on complex Clifford algebras. Although both approaches are formally equivalent, the hard-core boson formulation exhibits computational advantages. This work reviews and extends the hard-core boson algebra for circuit simulation and presents an efficient implementation. A performance comparison with IBM Qiskit shows substantially improved execution times for simulations. Moreover, a new application is introduced in which the hard-core boson formalism is combined with genetic algorithms for quantum circuit synthesis.

</details>


### [130] [Squeezing enhanced homodyne weak force sensing in cavity optomechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28007)
*Madan Mohan Mahana,Sanket Das,Tarak Nath Dey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究通过变分零差读出和量子压缩来突破腔光力系统的标准量子极限，提升弱力测量灵敏度，并证明腔内压缩和注入外部压缩均可进一步改善力灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔光力系统是量子传感的重要平台，但标准零差相位正交测量受标准量子极限限制，需要探索超越SQL的弱力测量方案。

Method: 采用变分零差正交读出与量子压缩（包括腔内压缩和注入外部压缩）相结合的方法，通过引入振幅与相位正交之间的量子关联来提升力灵敏度。

Result: 选择合适的零差角可显著提升力灵敏度；腔内压缩和注入外部压缩均可进一步改善灵敏度；两种方法均通过引入量子关联实现超越SQL的性能。

Conclusion: 压缩增强的变分零差探测方案可大幅提升腔光力传感器的力灵敏度，并有望推广到多种混合量子平台的高精度传感应用。

Abstract: Cavity optomechanical systems have emerged as a promising platform for quantum sensing. Quantum mechanics imposes a standard quantum limit (SQL) on the force-sensitivity for the standard homodyne phase quadrature measurement of the cavity's output field. In this paper, we investigate ways to enhance sensitivity beyond SQL for weak-force measurement by adopting a variational homodyne quadrature readout and quantum squeezing. Our study reveals a remarkable improvement in the force sensitivity of a cavity optomechanical sensor at a suitable homodyne angle, compared with standard phase quadrature detection of the cavity output field within a specific frequency band. We show that the force sensitivity can be further improved by intra-cavity squeezing (ICS) or injected external squeezing (IES) of the cavity mode. Both variational homodyne readout and quantum squeezing induce quantum correlation between the amplitude and phase quadratures of the cavity's output field, thereby improving the force sensitivity. The squeezing-enhanced variational homodyne detection scheme can enable high-precision sensing across various hybrid quantum platforms.

</details>


### [131] [Static features from mixing in short- and long-range Lindbladians: Markov property and correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28054)
*Paul Rosa-Ruiz,Matteo Scandi,Ángela Capel,Álvaro M. Alhambra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有长程相互作用的林德布拉德算符（Lindbladians）在满足快速混合和无挫条件时，其稳态的互信息（MI）和条件互信息（CMI）呈多项式衰减（而非指数衰减），并将短程系统的局部马尔可夫性质推广到长程非对易哈密顿量的吉布斯态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合态相的分类需要超越两点关联函数的判据（如MI和CMI的衰减）。理解林德布拉德算符稳态的静态性质如何从其生成元的动力学特征（快速混合和无挫性）中衍生出来，特别是在长程相互作用系统中。

Method: 理论证明：证明全局快速混合+无挫性→固定点CMI随屏蔽距离衰减；局部快速混合+本原性+正则性→全局MI衰减。通过数值示例（长程Ising模型，有无横场）验证。

Result: (i) 全局快速混合和无挫的局部林德布拉德算符，其固定点的CMI随屏蔽距离衰减；(ii) 局部快速混合+本原性+正则性导致全局MI衰减。对于幂律衰减($α$)的长程相互作用，两者均为多项式衰减（而非短程的指数衰减）。长程非对易哈密顿量的吉布斯态在任意温度下满足局部马尔可夫性质。

Conclusion: 长程相互作用系统的CMI和MI呈多项式衰减，对应无限马尔可夫长度，这与短程系统的指数衰减（有限马尔可夫长度）形成对比。数值上在长程Ising模型中观察到了CMI的多项式衰减。

Abstract: The classification of mixed-state phases requires criteria beyond two-point correlation functions, such as the decay of the mutual information (MI) and the conditional mutual information (CMI), with the latter encapsulated in the notion of Markov length. Here we show how such static properties of the fixed point of a Lindbladian follow from natural dynamical features of its generators: rapid mixing and frustration-freeness. We focus on systems with long-range interactions, and prove (i) that local Lindbladians satisfying (global) rapid mixing and frustration-freeness have fixed-points whose CMI decays with the shielding distance, and (ii) that (local) rapid mixing together with primitivity and regularity implies global decay of MI. For long-range interactions decaying with a power law with rate $α$, both quantities decay polynomially rather than exponentially, in contrast to the finite- and short-range regimes where exponential decay (a finite Markov length) is expected within a phase. We further show that Gibbs states of long-range, non-commuting Hamiltonians satisfy a local Markov property at any temperatures, extending the recent results (Chen--Rouzé, 2025) for short-range systems to the long-range regime relevant to a variety of experimental platforms. As a numerical example, we study the long-range Ising model both with and without a transverse field. We find regimes in which the polynomial decay of the CMI holds, in accordance with the bounds proven.

</details>


### [132] [Single Electrons in a Dual-Plane Printed-Circuit-Board Penning Trap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28078)
*Zirui Fang,Benedict A. D. Sukra,Xing Fan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了在二维可扩展双平面印刷电路板潘宁阱中实现单电子捕获与检测，表征了低磁场下的确定性电子加载、轴向阻尼、轴向温度及碰撞诱导磁控管半径增长，为平面潘宁陷阱建立了实用平台，并指明了向量子信息科学应用迈进的关键下一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种二维可扩展的平面潘宁阱平台，克服传统潘宁阱在可扩展性上的限制，为量子信息科学等应用提供实用化的基础。

Method: 采用双平面印刷电路板（PCB）设计的潘宁阱结构，实现二维可扩展性；通过实验表征确定性电子加载、轴向阻尼、轴向温度以及碰撞诱导的磁控管半径增长等关键性能指标，并在低磁场条件下进行测量。

Result: 成功实现了单电子的捕获与检测；在低磁场下完成了对电子加载的确定性控制、轴向阻尼特性、轴向温度以及磁控管半径增长的定量表征。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了平面潘宁阱作为实用平台的可行性，并指出了向量子信息科学应用发展的关键后续步骤。

Abstract: We demonstrate single-electron trapping and detection in a two-dimensionally scalable dual-plane printed-circuit-board Penning trap. We characterize deterministic electron loading, axial damping, axial temperature, and collision-induced magnetron-radius growth at low magnetic fields. These results establish a practical platform for planar Penning traps and identify key next steps toward applications in quantum information science.

</details>


### [133] [A Reproducible Pipeline for Symmetry-Respecting Excited States on Near-Term Quantum Computers: The H2O/STO-3G Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28130)
*Huajing Song*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过H₂O分子（12量子比特，STO-3G基组）的完整案例，系统分析了变分激发态量子算法在贫瘠高原、对称性污染、有限采样不稳定性和硬件成本等联合失效模式下的表现，并通过精确对角化验证所有结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常孤立分析变分激发态量子算法的各类失效机制（贫瘠高原、对称性污染、有限采样不稳定性、硬件成本），缺乏在统一框架下系统评估其耦合影响的完整工作。

Method: 以H₂O分子（STO-3G基组，12量子比特，Jordan-Wigner变换）为完整案例，对比了硬件高效拟设、数守恒拟设、ADAPT-VQE和量子运动方程（qEOM）子空间方法，结合精确对角化进行逐项验证，并引入矩阵感知的测量资源分配策略。

Result: 1）裸哈密顿量中阳离子态穿插在中性态之间；2）硬件高效和数守恒拟设停留在Hartree-Fock（Brillouin定理的精确驻点），ADAPT-VQE可逃脱；3）变分紧缩继承对称性污染并倒置能谱，qEOM子空间方法恢复亚毫哈特里的精度；4）粒子数保护在测量噪声下具有结构性；5）现实测量模型将数千子空间矩阵元压缩至约10⁵个对易群，矩阵感知分配策略约3×10⁹次总测量即可达到化学精度（比朴素逐元素估计低三个数量级）。

Conclusion: 单电路门保真度而非测量是当前的主要约束瓶颈；该工作作为教学和基准参考，所有代码、参数和图形均已开源发布。

Abstract: Variational excited-state quantum algorithms fail for reasons usually studied in isolation: barren plateaus, symmetry contamination, finite-sampling instability, and hardware cost. Using one small but complete system -- H$_2$O in the STO-3G basis (12 qubits, Jordan--Wigner) -- we assemble these into a single reproducible pipeline, checking every claim against exact diagonalization. The bare qubit Hamiltonian interleaves cation ($N{=}7$) states below the neutral manifold; hardware-efficient and number-conserving ansätze stall at Hartree--Fock, an exact stationary point by Brillouin's theorem, while ADAPT-VQE escapes; variational deflation inherits the contamination and inverts the spectrum, whereas the quantum equation-of-motion (qEOM) subspace method restores the ladder to sub-milli-Hartree accuracy. Particle number is protected \emph{structurally} under shot noise, and a realistic measurement model collapses the thousands of subspace matrix elements to $\sim\!10^5$ commuting groups; a matrix-aware shot allocation then reaches chemical accuracy at $\sim\!3\times10^9$ total shots -- a thousandfold below the naive per-element estimate and reachable in days -- leaving single-circuit gate fidelity, not measurement, as the binding constraint. This work is a teaching and benchmarking reference, not a new method; all code, parameters, and figures are released.

</details>


### [134] [Optimizing Resource Costs: A Practical Guide to Achieving Target Security in Verifiable Blind Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28139)
*Janice van Dam,Michał van Hooft,Stephanie D. C. Wehner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个优化框架，将可验证盲量子计算(VBQC)协议中参数选择问题形式化为约束优化问题，最小化协议轮数，并通过陷阱离子服务器案例展示了从硬件参数到协议参数和运行时估计的完整映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leichtle等人的噪声鲁棒VBQC协议涉及大量相互依赖的参数，在给定硬件噪声水平和安全目标下找到有效的参数集非常困难，缺乏系统化的参数选择方法。

Method: 将参数选择形式化为约束优化问题，开发实用求解框架，推导最小轮数的启发式公式，并考虑速率-保真度权衡以最小化端到端运行时。

Result: 框架能够输出给定设置下最小化轮数的协议参数；得到最小轮数的启发式公式；通过陷阱离子服务器案例展示了从客户端偏振控制硬件规格到协议参数和运行时估计的转换。

Conclusion: 该框架为近期的VBQC实现提供了具体的参数选择和资源估算指导，推动了可验证盲量子计算的实际部署。

Abstract: Verifiable blind quantum computing (VBQC) enables a resource-limited client to securely delegate computations to an untrusted quantum server while maintaining privacy and detecting deviations from the prescribed computation. The noise-robust VBQC protocol of Leichtle et al. achieves this through a round-based structure: the client delegates multiple computation rounds and test rounds, using the test outcomes to detect cheating while tolerating honest hardware noise. The protocol's security proof involves numerous interdependent parameters, making it non-trivial to find a valid parameter set for a given hardware noise level and security target. We formalize this as a constrained optimization problem and develop a practical framework to solve it. The framework yields the protocol parameters that minimize the number of rounds for any given setup. We derive a heuristic formula for the minimal number of rounds to help understand the scaling with noise and security targets and to provide rapid resource estimation. Since the number of rounds depends on noise while the time per round depends on hardware rate, the framework also enables optimization of rate-fidelity trade-offs to minimize end-to-end runtime. We demonstrate both applications through a case study of a trapped-ion server with a measurement-only client, showing how the client's polarization control hardware specifications translate into protocol parameters and runtime estimates, providing concrete guidance for near-term implementations.

</details>


### [135] [Time Evolution on Hybrid Tensor Networks -- A Novel and Parallelizable Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28169)
*Tobias V. Bauer,Richard M. Milbradt,Christian B. Mendl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于混合张量网络框架，提出了一种新的矩阵乘积态时间演化算法，将边界张量保留在经典计算机上，将高度纠缠的内部张量卸载到量子计算机上，采用BUG积分器演化经典张量，并实现经典与量子部分的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决矩阵乘积态时间演化中，当系统纠缠增长时经典内存需求过高的问题，通过将高度纠缠部分卸载到量子计算机来突破经典计算资源的瓶颈。

Method: 采用混合张量网络框架，使用BUG积分器时间演化经典张量，开发经典-量子耦合方案；量子部分可模块化集成任何量子时间演化方法（如预优化Trotter化）；经典和量子张量自由度比例可在时间演化中动态调整；经典和量子组件可在单时间步内并行运行，无需同步屏障或中途测量。

Result: 给出了针对矩阵乘积态张量网络的算法详细步骤和伪代码，证明了经典与量子部分可并行运行且不受同步限制。

Conclusion: 该混合算法能有效结合经典和量子计算资源，动态调整计算负载，为大规模张量网络时间演化提供可扩展的方案。

Abstract: We develop a novel time-evolution algorithm for matrix product states based on the recently introduced hybrid tensor network (hTN) framework. We retain the tensors close to the boundary on the classical computer and offload the highly entangled inner ones to the quantum computer. In our variant, we employ the Basis Update and Galerkin (BUG) integrator to time-evolve the classical tensors, and we develop a coupling scheme between the classical and quantum parts. Our framework admits modular combination with any quantum time-evolution method, such as (classically pre-optimized) Trotterization. The ratio of classical and quantum tensor degrees of freedom can be dynamically adjusted during the time evolution, which can be advantageous when the classical memory requirements become prohibitive. The quantum and classical components can run in parallel during a single time step and are not constrained by synchronization barriers or mid-circuit measurements. We describe the detailed steps and pseudocode for our algorithm specialized for tensor networks originating from the matrix product state Ansatz.

</details>


### [136] [Vacuum Fluctuation-Induced State Switching in Degenerate Optical Parametric Oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28178)
*Yihao Huang,Seou Choi,Rom Simovitch,Jamison Sloan,Charles Roques-Carmes,Michael Horodynski,Marin Soljačić,Yannick Salamin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了有偏置的光学参量振荡器（OPO）中，量子真空涨落如何通过噪声诱导宏观态切换，推导了平均切换时间的解析表达式，并分析了偏置场、泵浦增益和非线性对切换行为的影响，为噪声辅助的光子机器学习和概率量子门提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解双稳态驱动-耗散系统中量子真空涨落如何诱导宏观稳态之间的噪声驱动切换，以及如何通过外部偏置场控制这一微观量子涨落驱动的宏观跃迁过程。

Method: 通过构建有偏置的光学参量振荡器（OPO）模型，利用量子涨落理论推导平均切换时间的解析表达式，并通过模拟OPO场分布和态间概率流对解析结果进行验证。

Result: 成功推导了平均切换时间的解析公式，并通过仿真验证了其有效性；揭示了切换行为对偏置强度、泵浦增益和光学非线性的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 量子噪声可以塑造宏观动力学行为，该研究为噪声辅助的光子机器学习以及概率量子门的实现提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Bistable driven-dissipative systems near bifurcations can exhibit noise-activated switching between steady states. Here, we investigate how quantum vacuum fluctuations induce such switching in a biased optical parametric oscillator (OPO), a nonlinear system with intrinsic bistability. We show how microscopic quantum fluctuations driving macroscopic transitions can be controlled with an external bias field that reshapes the OPO steady-state metapotential. We derive analytical expressions for the average switching time and validate them through simulations of the OPO field distribution and inter-state probability flow under bias injection. We further examine how switching depends on bias strength, pump gain, and optical nonlinearity. Our findings clarify how quantum noise can shape macroscopic dynamics and provide a foundation for noise-assisted photonic machine learning and probabilistic quantum gates.

</details>


### [137] [Hybrid quantum-classical neural network for sample-efficient recognition of topological phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28199)
*Markus K. Hoffmann,Leon C. Sander,Colin Scarato,Christoph Hellings,Johannes Knörzer,Michael J. Hartmann,Petr Zapletal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种混合量子-经典神经网络，通过浅层参数化量子电路与经典神经网络联合训练，以更少的测量样本高效区分量子态拓扑相，相比经典方法将样本复杂度降低约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机输出态的全局特性表征面临测量成本过高的挑战，传统直接测量加经典后处理方法已不实用；现有量子神经网络需要深度量子电路，无法在现有量子设备上实现。

Method: 构建混合量子-经典神经网络：浅层参数化量子电路对测量基进行非局域变换，与经典神经网络联合训练，最大化不同量子态测量数据间的统计距离。采用监督学习区分表面码拓扑相、对称保护拓扑相及随机乘积态。

Result: 混合神经网络在识别拓扑相时，推理和训练样本复杂度均比基于随机泡利测量的经典神经网络降低约一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该混合架构采用可在现有量子计算机上实现的浅层量子电路，为复杂量子态的高效表征提供了可行方案。

Abstract: With increasing maturity of quantum computers, standard methods for characterizing global properties of their output quantum states via direct measurements and classical post-processing are becoming increasingly impractical due to large measurement costs. Although quantum neural networks could directly process quantum states to identify underlying characteristics with reduced measurement efforts, they often require deep quantum circuits that cannot be implemented on existing devices. To overcome these challenges, we introduce a hybrid quantum-classical neural network that consists of a shallow parameterized quantum circuit, measurements, and a classical neural network. The parameterized quantum circuit performs a nonlocal transformation of the measurement basis, which is jointly trained with the classical neural network to maximize the statistical distance between data obtained by measuring different quantum states. Using supervised learning, we demonstrate that the hybrid neural network distinguishes the topological phase of the surface code from a symmetry-enriched topological phase and random product states. Moreover, this hybrid neural network reduces both inference and training sample complexities of recognizing the topological phase by approximately one order of magnitude compared to a classical neural network trained on randomized Pauli measurements. As this hybrid neural network features a shallow quantum circuit that can be readily implemented on existing quantum computers, it enables the efficient characterization of complex quantum states.

</details>


### [138] [Hybrid Quantum-Classical Neural Networks for Recognizing Quantum Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28201)
*Colin Scarato,Johannes Knörzer,Markus K. Hoffmann,Leon C. Sander,Luca Hofele,Shengpu Wang,Kilian Hanke,Ashay Sathe,Dominic Hagmann,Alexander Flasby,Michael J. Hartmann,Petr Zapletal,Andreas Wallraff,Christoph Hellings*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一种混合量子-经典神经网络，用于量子相位识别。该网络结合了硬件高效的参数化量子电路和前馈神经网络，并在超导量子硬件上进行联合训练。实验展示了在磁场中对最多4x4格点表面码晶格的量子相位分类器，能够区分拓扑基态与随机乘积态，单次测量准确率超过85%，十次测量平均准确率超过99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别物质的量子相位是理解强关联材料的关键，但这对传统计算机和当前量子处理器都是一项具有挑战性的任务。

Method: 提出一种混合量子-经典神经网络架构，将硬件高效的参数化量子电路与经典前馈神经网络结合，在超导量子硬件上进行联合优化训练，实现对表面码晶格量子相位的分类。

Result: 训练的混合分类器能够区分拓扑基态与随机选择的乘积态，即使受到任意单量子比特泡利错误干扰时仍有效。单次测量准确率超过85%，十次测量平均准确率超过99%。

Conclusion: 混合量子-经典神经网络在经典方法样本复杂度呈不利缩放的情景下，是一种有前景的量子态表征方法。

Abstract: Identifying quantum phases of matter is key to understanding strongly correlated materials, but remains a challenging task for both conventional computers and current quantum processors. Here, we introduce and implement a hybrid quantum-classical neural network for quantum phase recognition by combining a hardware-efficient parameterized quantum circuit and a feedforward neural network. We jointly train both components with superconducting quantum hardware in the optimization loop, to experimentally demonstrate a classifier for the quantum phases of surface code lattices with up to 4x4 sites in a magnetic field. To learn nonlocal features of the topological phase, we train the hybrid neural network to distinguish topological ground states of the surface code from a featureless ensemble of product states. This allows the trained classifier to distinguish topological ground states from randomly chosen product states, even when subjected to any single-qubit Pauli error. The classifier reaches accuracies above 85% in single-shot measurements, and above 99% when averaging over ten measurements. We expect hybrid neural networks such as the one presented here to be a promising approach for characterizing quantum states in scenarios where classical methods exhibit an unfavorable scaling of sample complexity.

</details>


### [139] [Noise-Directed Adaptive Remapping for Integer Optimization: from qubits to (encoded) qudits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28234)
*Stuart Hadfield,Filip B. Maciejewski,Davide Venturelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将噪声导向自适应重映射（NDAR）从无约束二元优化扩展到离散整数域上的优化问题，引入了额外的逻辑层规范自由度，分析了不同编码（qudit原生、二元、one-hot、domain-wall）在NDAR框架下的可行性与权衡，并以Max-k-colorable子图问题为例进行了论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将NDAR这一利用器件噪声作为计算资源的启发式元算法从二元优化推广到更一般的离散整数域优化问题，使其适用于更广泛的组合优化场景。

Method: 通过引入逻辑层规范自由度，使得每次迭代的规范变换不再唯一；分析了NDAR超二元域所需的编码依赖条件（噪声吸引子可行性、兼容规范变换的存在性、变换选择策略），并针对qudit原生、二元、one-hot、domain-wall四种编码进行了比较分析。

Result: 不同编码在NDAR框架下展现出不同的优势与权衡，噪声诱导动力学与解空间及编码选择之间存在复杂交互；NDAR引导的噪声考虑为比较器件级编码选择提供了新标准。

Conclusion: NDAR框架为量子优化中的编码选择提供了新的噪声感知视角，未来可向超导qudit器件实验实现及算法改进方向推进。

Abstract: We extend Noise-Directed Adaptive Remapping (NDAR), a recently proposed heuristic meta-algorithm that leverages device noise as a computational resource, to optimization problems over discrete (integer) domains. While originally introduced for unconstrained binary optimization, the proposed generalization introduces additional gauge degrees of freedom at the logical level, such that the gauge transformation applied at each iteration is no longer unique, allowing tailoring to particular encodings or quantum hardware. We identify encoding-dependent requirements for NDAR beyond binary domains: feasibility of the noise attractor, existence of compatible gauge transformations that preserve an efficiently implementable circuit family, and a systematic way to select the transform to apply at each step. We analyze these criteria for qudit-native and for binary, one-hot, and domain-wall qubit encodings, using the Max-k-colorable subgraph problem as a running example. We demonstrate that these encodings can exhibit distinct advantages and tradeoffs when integrated within the NDAR framework, particularly in how noise-induced dynamics interact with the solution landscape and choice of encoding. Our results indicate that NDAR-guided noise considerations provide a new criterion for comparing device-level encoding choices for quantum optimization. Finally, we outline directions toward experimental realization in superconducting qudit devices and further algorithmic improvements.

</details>


### [140] [Qudit extension of parameterized IQP circuits: A generative quantum machine learning approach to integer data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28236)
*Robert J. Banks,Arianna Crippa,Matthias Traube,Josua Unger,Christian Ertler,Wolfgang Lechner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对参数化瞬时量子多项式（IQP）电路在处理非二元数据时的局限性，提出了一种基于qudit（高维量子系统）的扩展方法。该方法将整数值像素映射为固定长度比特串，改造IQP电路以适应qudit形式，并开发了适用于电路训练的损失函数及特征协方差矩阵计算方法。在CLIC探测器电磁量能器的单粒子电子簇射能量沉积数据上进行了验证，结果表明该方法可有效处理非二元数据，并可推广至其他量子生成式机器学习应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化IQP电路在二元数据分布上表现良好，但处理非二元数据集时存在明显局限：将整数值映射为与量子比特兼容的二元表示往往会破坏数据原有的度量结构。因此需要将IQP电路扩展至qudit形式，以直接处理整数映射的数据，保留数据的原始度量信息。

Method: 将IQP电路适配为qudit形式：每个整数值像素被编码为固定长度比特串，量子门根据qudit形式进行变换。同时开发了适用于电路训练的损失函数以及特征间协方差矩阵的计算方法。

Result: 在CLIC探测器电磁量能器的单粒子电子簇射能量沉积数据上验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于qudit的IQP电路扩展方法能够有效处理非二元数据，并可推广至其他利用量子生成式机器学习处理非二元数据的应用场景。

Abstract: Parameterized Instantaneous Quantum Polynomial (IQP) circuits have proven useful in quantum generative learning models, particularly for binary distributions. However, when applied to non-binary datasets, they exhibit notable limitations: mapping integer values into qubit-compatible binary representations often destroys the original metric structure of the data. In this paper we aim to extend them to a qudits formulation operating on an integer mapping of the data. The IQP quantum circuit is adapted to encode each integer valued pixel into a bit-string of fixed length and quantum gates are transformed to follow the qudit formalism. As a generative machine learning approach, a suitable loss function for the circuit training and the calculation of the covariance matrix among features are developed and validated on the energy deposits from single-particle electron showers in the electromagnetic calorimeter of the CLIC detector. The method proposed in this work can be also extended to other applications that utilize quantum generative machine learning for non-binary data.

</details>


### [141] [Parameter-Efficient Continuous-Variable Photonic Quantum Neural Networks for Edge Quantum AI: Demonstration in Oral Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28252)
*Akshay Bhagwan Sonawane,Sophie Choe,Lakshman Tamil*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合经典-连续变量（CV）光量子分类器，用于从智能手机图像中检测口腔癌。该流水线结合MobileNetV1特征提取器、PCA降维至16维，以及参数化的CV-QNN架构（包含位移、干涉和Kerr门）。作者提出简化的Φ∘D∘U₁ CV-QNN结构，相比标准CV-QNN层减少40-45%可训练参数，并采用降维和编码限制策略缓解贫瘠高原问题，将损失梯度方差提升约58个数量级。四qumode简化CV-QNN模型仅含18个参数，验证AUC最高，超过含55个参数的经典基线（参数减少67%），并在所有随机种子下达到100%校准测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口腔癌早期检测能显著改善临床结果，但在资源匮乏地区缺乏专业诊断工具。基于智能手机的筛查是一种可扩展方案，但需要能在边缘硬件约束下运行的轻量级模型。混合经典-量子架构是参数高效学习的新兴方向，但多数依赖需要低温运行的量子比特硬件，不适合边缘部署。室温运行的连续变量光量子计算提供了一种互补路径。

Method: 提出混合经典-CV量子分类器：MobileNetV1提取特征→PCA降至16维→参数化CV-QNN（位移、干涉、Kerr门）。提出简化Φ∘D∘U₁架构减少参数40-45%，并采用降维和编码限制策略缓解贫瘠高原。

Result: 简化层在四qumode下显著优于全层（使用44%更少参数）；四qumode简化CV-QNN（18参数）验证AUC最高，超过55参数经典基线（参数减少67%），所有种子下达到100%校准测试准确率。

Conclusion: CV光量子机器学习可用于参数高效、室温运行的医学图像分类，推动了边缘量子AI的发展。

Abstract: Early detection of oral cancer markedly improves clinical outcomes, yet specialized diagnostic tools remain scarce in low-resource settings. Smartphone-based screening is a scalable alternative but needs lightweight models that run within edge-hardware constraints. Hybrid classical-quantum architectures are emerging candidates for parameter-efficient learning, yet most rely on qubit hardware that needs cryogenic operation, unsuitable for edge deployment. Continuous-variable (CV) photonic quantum computing, which operates at room temperature, offers a complementary route. We investigate a hybrid classical-CV quantum classifier for oral cancer detection from smartphone images. The pipeline combines a MobileNetV1 feature extractor, principal component analysis to 16 dimensions, and a parameterized CV-QNN of displacement, interferometric, and Kerr gates on a photonic backend. We propose a simplified $Φ\circ D \circ U_1$ CV-QNN architecture that cuts trainable parameters 40-45% relative to the standard CV-QNN layer of Killoran et al. (2019a), and identify dimensionality-reduction and encoding-restriction strategies that mitigate barren plateaus, raising loss-gradient variance by roughly 58 orders of magnitude. Whether the simplified layer beats the full layer is width-dependent: the full layer holds a small but significant edge at two qumodes, whereas the simplified layer is significantly better at four qumodes using 44% fewer parameters. The strongest model, a four-qumode simplified CV-QNN with only 18 parameters, attains the highest validation AUC of all models, exceeds a 55-parameter classical baseline using 67% fewer parameters, and reaches 100% calibrated test accuracy across all seeds. These results support CV photonic quantum machine learning for parameter-efficient, room-temperature medical image classification and motivate progress toward edge quantum AI.

</details>


### [142] [Efficient targeting of arbitrary excited states with quantum inverse power iteration through filtering polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28255)
*Srushti Patil,Nina Glaser*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子奇异值变换(QSVT)的量子逆幂迭代(QIPI)算法，用于制备任意激发态。通过对比切比雪夫分解和本征态滤波(EF)两种变换技术，发现EF方法由于滤波多项式的对称性，在鲁棒性上显著优于其他方法。数值模拟显示该方法在收敛性和高阶激发态获取方面表现优异，在容错量子计算场景下具有较低的T门资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子逆幂方法（如傅里叶分解+数值求积）对超参数高度敏感且数值不稳定，实际仅适用于基态制备。为实现鲁棒的激发态制备，需探索替代变换技术。

Method: 提出基于QSVT的QIPI算法：通过能量平移ω，迭代应用(H-ωI)^{-1}的近似到试探态。研究了两种变换技术——切比雪夫逆分解(Cheb-inv)和基于QSVT的本征态滤波(EF)，并分析了各自的鲁棒性和收敛性。

Result: 在H₂、LiH、BeH₂分子哈密顿量的数值模拟中，EF基QIPI相比其他量子幂方法在收敛性和高阶激发态获取方面更优。容错逻辑资源估计显示，该方法能以适中的多项式次数实现高目标态放大。

Conclusion: EF-QIPI因其滤波多项式的对称性可避免发散并有效抑制非目标本征态，是容错量子化学中可扩展的激发态制备方案。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce a quantum inverse power iteration (QIPI) algorithm based on the quantum singular value transformation (QSVT) to target arbitrary excited states. Given an energy shift $ω$, QIPI prepares the target excited state by iteratively applying an approximation of the shifted inverse Hamiltonian $(H-ωI)^{-1}$ to a trial state. Prior quantum inverse power approaches typically relied on Fourier decompositions of the inverse Hamiltonian, with numerical quadrature used to reconstruct the transformation, but such methods are highly sensitive to hyperparameter choices and have been observed to be numerically unstable, effectively restricting their use to ground-state preparation. To enable robust excited-state targeting, we investigate two alternative transformation techniques: a Chebyshev decomposition of the inverse (Cheb-inv) and an eigenstate filtering (EF) approach based on QSVT. We find that EF-based QIPI is substantially more robust than Cheb-inv and other decomposition-based approaches due to the symmetry of the applied filtering polynomial, avoiding divergence with respect to the choice of $ω$ and efficiently suppressing off-target eigenstates even in closely spaced spectra. Numerical simulations for molecular Hamiltonians of H$_2$, LiH, and BeH$_2$ show improved convergence and enhanced access to higher excited states relative to other quantum power methods. Assuming standard oracle access to the Hamiltonian, we further provide logical resource estimates in fault-tolerant settings in terms of T gate counts, and conclude that QIPI can achieve high target state amplification with modest polynomial degrees, thereby making it a promising candidate for scalable excited-state preparation in fault-tolerant quantum chemistry applications.

</details>


### [143] [Efficient Approximation of the Wigner Kernel in Phase-Space Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28269)
*Mehran Attar,Bassant Selim,Jean Michel Sellier*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于级数展开的Wigner核解析近似方法，用于一维单体量子系统的符号粒子公式框架，通过高斯势剖面验证，在保持主要行为的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在符号粒子公式中，Wigner核的计算涉及高度振荡积分，在时变模拟中计算开销极大，亟需高效近似方法以提升可扩展性。

Method: 利用势函数基于级数表示的解析展开，推导出Wigner核及Gamma函数的近似表达式，避免直接计算高度振荡积分。

Result: 在单/双/三/四高斯势剖面上，近似方法与精确直接计算的结果在主要行为上吻合，同时计算成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 所提近似方法可作为符号粒子公式量子模拟的高效计算组件，适用于可扩展的量子仿真。

Abstract: The Signed Particle Formulation provides a particle-based interpretation of quantum mechanics in phase space, where quantum dynamics are represented through the creation and evolution of signed particles. A central computational challenge in this framework is the evaluation of the Wigner kernel, which generally involves highly oscillatory integrals and can become computationally demanding in time-dependent simulations. This paper proposes an analytical approximation of the Wigner kernel for one-dimensional single body quantum systems by exploiting a series-based representation of the potential function. The resulting expression provides an efficient way to approximate the Wigner kernel and the associated Gamma function, which governs the particle-generation process in the Signed Particle Formulation framework. The proposed approximation is evaluated for several Gaussian-based potential profiles, including single, double, triple, and quadruple Gaussian potentials. Numerical comparisons between the approximated and directly computed Wigner kernels and Gamma functions show that the proposed method captures the main behavior of the exact quantities while significantly reducing the computational cost. These results indicate that the proposed approximation can serve as an efficient computational component for scalable Signed Particle Formulation based quantum simulations.

</details>


### [144] [Composing Quantum Instruments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28291)
*Robert I. Booth,Dominik Leichtle,Alex Rice,Kim Worrall*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了经典控制的量子仪器（量子马尔可夫核的自然类比）的复合，通过Okamura-Ozawa正规扩张在冯·诺依曼张量积上构造了量子信道值函数关于量子仪器的积分，建立了复合的幺半群结构，并将量子马尔可夫核范畴识别为该幺半群的Kleisli范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典马尔可夫核通过积分复合，而具有连续结果的量子仪器复合需要定义量子信道值函数关于量子仪器的积分，现有理论缺乏此构造。

Method: 采用Heisenberg图像，利用Okamura-Ozawa正规扩张到冯·诺依曼张量积，构造量子信道值函数关于量子仪器的积分。

Result: 该积分恢复了预期的有限公式，保持了正规完全正性和次幺正性，并为量子仪器的复合提供了幺半群乘法。

Conclusion: 将量子马尔可夫核范畴识别为该幺半群的Kleisli范畴，建立了量子仪器复合的范畴论基础。

Abstract: We study the composition of classically-controlled quantum instruments--the natural quantum analogue of Markov kernels. Classically, Markov kernels compose by integrating one kernel against another. Defining this composition for quantum instruments with continuous outcomes requires an integral of quantum channel-valued functions with respect to a quantum instrument. We construct this integral in the Heisenberg picture using the Okamura-Ozawa normal extension to a von Neumann tensor product. This integral recovers the expected finite formula, preserves normal complete positivity and subunitality, and provides the multiplication for a monad governing the composition of quantum instruments. As an immediate consequence, we identify the category of quantum Markov kernels as the Kleisli category of this monad.

</details>


### [145] [Diameter truncated operator evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28313)
*Tom Holden-Dye,Max Marvell,Joel Mills,Christoper J. Turner,Arijeet Pal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于算子直径（而非权重）的截断方法，用于高效模拟非平衡量子系统中的算子动力学，并在 kicked Ising 模型和 Heisenberg XXZ 模型中验证了其有效性，能够准确提取局域关联函数和输运性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡量子系统中算子复杂度的快速增长使得精确模拟通常难以实现。尽管已有基于低权重截断的方法取得了进展，但需要探索更高效的截断策略来进一步降低计算成本，同时保持对局域关联函数和输运性质的准确描述。

Method: 提出基于算子直径（算子在格点上非平凡支撑的区域大小）的截断协议，并利用已有的通用电路分析论证该截断的物理合理性。在 kicked Ising 模型和 Heisenberg XXZ 模型上进行大规模数值模拟验证其效果。

Result: 数值结果表明，基于直径的截断协议能够高效且准确地提取局域关联函数和输运性质，在多个感兴趣系统中表现有效。

Conclusion: 基于直径的截断是一种物理上合理且计算高效的算子模拟方法，为处理非平衡量子系统动力学提供了一种新的有效工具。

Abstract: We present a method for simulating operator dynamics in out-of-equilibrium quantum systems. Due to the rapid growth of complexity in these systems, this is typically speaking an intractable task. However, exceptional progress has been made in recent years to sidestep this barrier, with the introduction of a number of methods that make use of a truncation of the simulation to low-weight (the number of non-trivial terms in a Pauli string basis expansion) observables, which turns out to be a good approximation for many dynamical quantities of interest, e.g., two-point infinite-temperature correlation functions between local operators. In this work, we extend this idea to a leaner truncation protocol, truncating operators based on their diameter - that is, the size of the region on the lattice on which they are non-trivially supported. Using existing analysis for generic circuits we argue that this kind of truncation protocol is physically well-motivated, and show via extensive numerical simulations for a number of systems of interest (here, the kicked Ising model and the Heisenberg XXZ model) that it is effective, and allows us to efficiently and accurately extract local correlation functions and transport properties.

</details>
